---
title: "CLI Commands"
---

This page is generated from the help of the two CLIs: `flint` (your Flints) and `nuu` (the identity of this machine, the NUU account, the org, and the network). Do not edit it by hand. To make it again, run `node scripts/gen-cli-reference.mjs --out <this file>` in the repo `flint`. `--check <this file>` fails when a command or a flag of the help is missing here.

Each command also prints its help: `flint <command> --help`, `nuu <command> --help`.

The Obsidian rows that other commands print, and the Obsidian commands that 0.7.0 removed, are in [Obsidian in Other Commands](/flint/reference/obsidian-in-other-commands).

## flint

```text
flint [options] [command]
```

Make and manage Flints: folders of notes and shards

Subcommands: `create`, `list`, `browse`, `status`, `open`, `open-url`, `cd`, `rename`, `convert`, `org`, `obshn`, `register`, `unregister`, `delete`, `move`, `sync`, `plan`, `registry`, `update`, `source`, `workspace`, `reference`, `fulfill`, `resolve`, `entity`, `worktree`, `send`, `shard`, `module`, `config`, `obsidian`, `migrate`, `plate`, `orbh`, `orbtest`, `orbcode`, `ite`, `tinderbox`, `server`, `steel`, `orbit`, `blacksmith`, `setup`, `doctor`, `fix`, `feedback`, `git`, `helper`, `mcp`, `features`, `where`, `env`, `whoami`, `auth-status`, `login`, `logout`, `foundation`.

### flint create

```text
flint create|init [options] <name>
```

Create a Flint: a folder with a Mesh, shards, a Git repository, and an Obsidian vault

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The name of the Flint |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | The parent folder (default: this folder) |
| `--preset <name>` | The first shards (default: blank). See the presets below |
| `--org <slug\|none>` | The org (default: the default org of this machine, or none) |
| `--org-id <uuid>` | The id of that org, when this machine does not know it |
| `--visibility <level>` | private \| org \| public (default: org with an org, else private) |
| `--no-obsidian` | Skip Obsidian for this run only (flint config set obsidian off turns it off on this machine) |
| `--open` | Open the Flint in Obsidian now (default: only in a terminal and without --json) |
| `--no-open` | Do not launch Obsidian now; the payload is installed |
| `--no-git` | No Git repository |
| `--from <url>` | Clone a Flint from Git (its files give the name and the org) |
| `--resume <path>` | Complete a creation that stopped |
| `--type <type>` | guide or member (see the types below) |
| `--about <address>` | Type guide: the entity that the Guide is about |
| `--regarding <org>` | Type member: the org slug or id |
| `--verbose` | Show every step, the shard install lines, and the kernel changes |
| `--json` | One JSON result on stdout. The progress goes to stderr |

```text
A Flint name uses the letters A to Z, the digits 0 to 9, spaces, and the characters - ' , . ! & + ; @. It has at most 100 characters. It does not end with a space or a dot.

Presets:
  blank        flint, orbh                     (default) Ask the agent what the Flint is for
  garden       flint, orbh, notepad
  quarry       flint, orbh, notepad
  lab          flint, orbh, notepad, plan
  observatory  flint, orbh, reports
  factory      flint, orbh, projects, increments, reports
  default      flint, orbh, and 7 shards for projects, plans, and reports

Types (most Flints need no type):
  --type guide   --about <address>             A Guide about an entity
  --type member  --regarding <org>             Your Member Flint of an org
  flint setup always makes the Computer Flint. flint create does not make it.

Examples:
  flint create "My Notes"
  flint create "Team Notes" --org none
  flint create "Server Notes" --no-obsidian
  flint create --from git@github.com:owner/notes.git
```

### flint list

```text
flint list|ls [options]
```

List the Flints of the roster of this machine

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | One JSON line: \{ ok, kind: "list", flints: [...] \} |
| `--full-path` | Show full paths (not abbreviated) |
| `-s, --status` | Show valid/broken status for each entry |
| `-g, --group` | Group the Flints by tag |
| `-t, --tag <tag>` | Show only the Flints with this tag |
| `--search <query>` | Show only the Flints whose name or description has this text |

### flint browse

```text
flint browse [options]
```

Browse the Flints of the roster, and open the Orbh cockpit in one of them

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--all` | Include registry entries whose folder is missing on disk |
| `--no-kitty-keyboard` | Do not enable the kitty keyboard protocol for Shift-Enter |

### flint status

```text
flint status [options]
```

Show the CLI release, this machine, this Flint, and pending migrations

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value with every fact |
| `--id` | Print only the id of this Flint |

### flint open

```text
flint open [options] [reference] [name]
```

Open or focus a Flint in Obsidian

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `reference` | A Flint name or reference, such as "NUU Flint" or "(Guide) NUU Cognition". The word computer is the Computer Flint of this machine. With a second argument: a type |
| `name` | The name, when the first argument is a type: flint open guide "NUU Cognition" |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-launch` | Resolve and print the target without opening Obsidian |

### flint open-url

```text
flint open-url [options] <url>
```

Open the target of a flint:// action URL

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `url` | A flint://open?ref=… action URL |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-launch` | Resolve and print the target without opening Obsidian |

### flint cd

```text
flint cd [options] <reference> [name]
```

Change the shell directory to a registered Flint

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `reference` | A Flint name or reference, such as "NUU Flint" or "(Guide) NUU Cognition". The word computer is the Computer Flint of this machine. With a second argument: a type |
| `name` | The name, when the first argument is a type: flint cd guide "NUU Cognition" |

### flint rename

```text
flint rename [options] <new-name>
```

Rename the Flint containing the current directory. In the Computer Flint, the machine display name changes too

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `new-name` | The new Display Name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output one JSON line |

### flint convert

```text
flint convert [options] <flint>
```

Change the type of a Flint: flint, computer, guide, member. The id does not change

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `flint` | The Flint: a name, an address, or an id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--to <type>` | The new type: flint, computer, guide, or member |
| `--abort` | Return a conversion that stopped part way to its start |
| `--dry-run` | Print the plan and change nothing |
| `--owner <uuid>` | To member: the id of the person (default: person-id of the home config) |
| `--regarding <org>` | To member: the org that the Flint regards (slug or id) |
| `--regarding-id <uuid>` | To member: the id of that org, when this machine does not know it |
| `--about <address>` | To guide: the entity that the guide is about |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line |

### flint org

```text
flint org [options] [command]
```

The org of this Flint, bound by id

Subcommands: `set`, `show`.

#### flint org set

```text
flint org set [options] <org>
```

Set the org of this Flint. The id comes from --id, the enrollment of this machine, or a Flint of this machine with that org. `none` removes the org: a Flint with no org is local (`@/`)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `org` | The org slug, the org id, or none |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--id <uuid>` | The id of the org. The admin of the org gives it. Not needed when this machine knows the org |
| `--dry-run` | Print the plan and change nothing |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line |

#### flint org show

```text
flint org show [options]
```

Show the org of this Flint: the slug of flint.toml and the id of flint.json

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output one JSON line |

### flint obshn

```text
flint obshn [options] <orbh-profile> [prompt]
```

Open a new terminal tab in Obsidian and launch `flint orbh i <orbh-profile>`

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `orbh-profile` | Orbh profile target (e.g. claude/o55h); '' lets Orbh select from its default target list |
| `prompt` | Optional initial prompt for the agent |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <flint>` | The Flint: a folder, or a reference as in flint open (default: auto-detect) |
| `--at <scope>` | Orbh store scope for the new session ('machine' or 'flint:`<name>`'); default is the vault's own store |
| `--account <name>` | The harness account of the new session (default: the effective account of the runtime) |

### flint register

```text
flint register [options] [path]
```

Register a Flint in the roster (places.json). A registered Flint takes the name and type of flint.toml again

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `path` | Path to a flint to register (defaults to current directory) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-y, --yes` | Ask no question (register asks none; UX5 gives every command the flag) |
| `--json` | Output one JSON value |

### flint unregister

```text
flint unregister [options] [path]
```

Unregister a Flint from the roster (places.json)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `path` | Path to the flint (defaults to current directory) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-y, --yes` | Ask no question |
| `-f, --force` | The same as --yes |
| `--json` | Output one JSON value. It asks no question, so it needs --yes |

### flint delete

```text
flint delete [options] [name-or-path]
```

Delete a Flint for good: its folder, its row in the roster, and its vault registry entry. The Obsidian backups stay

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name-or-path` | Flint name or path (defaults to current directory) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-f, --force` | Skip confirmation prompt |

### flint move

```text
flint move [options] <name-or-path> <destination>
```

Move a Flint and update the roster

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name-or-path` | A Flint name or reference (resolved through the roster, as open does), or a path to a Flint |
| `destination` | Destination path |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-f, --force` | Overwrite destination if it already exists |
| `-y, --yes` | Skip the Tinderbox-membership confirmation prompt |

### flint sync

```text
flint sync [options]
```

Make the files of this Flint match its lock and its declarations

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--migrate` | Apply pending script migrations before sync |
| `--dry-run` | Show the complete sync plan |
| `--no-git` | Not needed: flint sync does not touch Git (retired) |
| `--no-update` | Not needed: flint sync does not move the lock (retired) |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as JSON |

### flint plan

```text
flint plan [options]
```

Show the same preview as flint sync --dry-run

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--no-git` | Not needed: flint sync does not touch Git (retired) |
| `--no-update` | Not needed: flint sync does not move the lock (retired) |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as JSON |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

### flint update

```text
flint update [options]
```

Update flint-cli to the newest release (the npm dist-tag latest)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-c, --check` | Check for a newer release without installing |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value: the current version, the newest version, and the install kind; install nothing |

### flint source

```text
flint source [options] [command]
```

Manage external sources (repositories)

Subcommands: `repo`.

#### flint source repo

```text
flint source repo [options] [command]
```

Manage source repositories

Subcommands: `add`, `remove`, `update`, `list`.

##### flint source repo add

```text
flint source repo add [options] <name> <url>
```

Declare a source repository in flint.toml — a reference copy is cloned into Sources/Repos/ by `flint sync`

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Name for this source repository (Proper Case) |
| `url` | Git URL to clone |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint source repo remove

```text
flint source repo remove [options] <name>
```

Remove a source repository

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Name of the source repository to remove |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-f, --force` | Skip confirmation prompt |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint source repo update

```text
flint source repo update [options] <name>
```

Re-fetch a source repository (delete and re-clone)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Name of the source repository to update |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint source repo list

```text
flint source repo list [options]
```

List source repositories

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

### flint workspace

```text
flint workspace [options] [command]
```

```text
flint workspace create [options] <name> <remote>
```

Declare a Workspace/Repos/ repository in flint.toml — materialized (cloned from the remote) by `flint sync`

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Name for this repository (Proper Case) |
| `remote` | Git remote URL to set as origin |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |


| Argument | Description |
|---|---|

| `remote` | Git remote URL to clone from |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint workspace register

```text

```

Register an existing folder under Workspace/Repos/ in flint.toml, using its git origin remote

|---|---|

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

### flint fulfill

```text
flint fulfill [options] <type> [name] [targetPath]
```

Fulfill a codebase reference with a local path

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `type` | Resource type: codebase |
| `name` | Name of the resource to fulfill (or --all) |
| `targetPath` | Local path to the resource |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--target <path>` | Target path (alternative to positional argument) |
| `--all` | Fulfill all unfulfilled codebase references |

### flint resolve

```text
flint resolve [options] <ref-or-type> [legacy-name]
```

Resolve an entity reference through the machine router, a shard package (@org/shard/name[@range][#place]), or a legacy Flint reference

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref-or-type` | Entity reference, such as "(Guide) NUU Cognition"; or a type (codebase, flint, bundle, source, or an entity type such as guide) |
| `legacy-name` | The name, when the first argument is a type |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint for legacy reference resolution |
| `--verb <verb>` | The read verb: lookup, describe, locate, children (default: "lookup") |
| `--to <rung>` | The rung to ask for: ping or locate (default: "locate") |
| `--fresh` | Bypass the cache |
| `--max-age <seconds>` | Accept a cached answer up to this age (the cache is off without it) |
| `--net` | Resolve through nclient, for any reference. A reference whose #place is another machine always goes through nclient |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

### flint shard

```text
flint shard [options] [command] [name] [action] [args...]
```

Manage shards — install, create, and maintain shard packages

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Shard ref to show its status, or use with exec |
| `action` | Action: `<script-name>`, exec `<script>` |
| `args` | Action arguments |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

Subcommands: `install`, `uninstall`, `reinstall`, `list`, `browse`, `update`, `broadcast`, `status`, `migrate`, `create`, `build`, `type`, `dev`, `clone`, `fork`, `rename`, `id`, `setup`, `start`, `start-dev`, `hstart`, `hstart-dev`, `push`, `pull`, `publish`, `unpublish`, `published`, `release`, `versions`, `scripts`.

#### flint shard install

```text
flint shard install [options] [sources...]
```

Make the lock match the specs: resolve each spec, record the registry answers, install what is missing. With a source: install one or more shards (a package spec @org/name[@range][#place], a bare name, the name of a shard source, a path, or owner/repo). With --core: install each missing core shard

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `sources` | Package specs (e.g. @nuucognition/notepad@^1.1, notepad), the names of shard sources, paths, or owner/repo |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-f, --force` | Overwrite existing shard files |
| `--all-dev` | Build the shard of every shard source |
| `--core` | Install each core shard (Flint, Orbh) that this Flint does not have; leave the present ones alone |
| `--no-deps` | Install the shard alone: do not install its missing dependencies first |
| `--with-deps` | Install the missing dependencies first (the default) |
| `--from-git <owner/repo[#ref]>` | Install from a Git location (a tag, a branch, or a commit after #); the registry names the package and its state |
| `--from-path <dir>` | Install from a folder on this machine (a path record) |
| `--alias <alias>` | The local name of the shard in this Flint (the key in flint.toml); the slug of the package by default. Needed for a second shard with the same slug. |
| `--reference` | Install by reference (use = "reference"): no copy under Shards/; the loader reads the source folder on this machine. Not for a GitHub source. |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--dry-run` | With no source: show the plan and the registry answers, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Output one JSON object with retained files (with no source: the operation report) |

#### flint shard uninstall

```text
flint shard uninstall [options] <name>
```

Uninstall a shard and remove its unchanged payload files

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--json` | Output one JSON object with removed and kept files |

#### flint shard reinstall

```text
flint shard reinstall [options] [name]
```

Reinstall installed shards from their declared source. With no name, reinstalls all.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Shorthand of a single installed shard to reinstall |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--replace-note-templates` | Replace each changed note template with the note template of the shard. A backup of the changed file comes first. |
| `--json` | Output one JSON object with retained files |

#### flint shard list

```text
flint shard list|ls [options]
```

List shards: one row per shard, with its id, package name, shorthand, version, and state (and the alias when it differs from the slug)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-s, --sources` | Show only the shard records of flint.toml |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint shard browse

```text
flint shard browse|search [options] [query]
```

Browse the shards that this Flint can install: the registry and the sources of the Flints of this machine

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `query` | Show only the shards whose name, slug, shorthand, address, or description has this text |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--installed` | Show only the shards that this Flint has |
| `--available` | Show only the shards that this Flint does not have |
| `--json` | Print one value: the query, the entries, and the warnings |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |
| `--verbose` | Show the fault behind a warning |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard update

```text
flint shard update [options] [ref]
```

Move the lock inside the range of each spec: the pnpm update of Flint. Updates every installed shard, or one shard by its alias, shorthand, or id; a build of a source is skipped

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The shard to update: the package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id. Default: every installed shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--reinstall` | Force re-download and reinstall all installed shards regardless of version |
| `--json` | Output one JSON object with retained files |

#### flint shard broadcast

```text
flint shard broadcast [options] <ref>
```

Update a shard in each Flint of this machine that installs it from this Flint (#\<this Flint>); run it after a change to the source

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The shard of this Flint: the package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Show the consumers and what the broadcast would do; write nothing |
| `--only <flint...>` | Only these consumer Flints: a name, the slug of a name, or a path |
| `--json` | Print one value: the shard and one row for each consumer |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard status

```text
flint shard status|info [options] <ref>
```

Show one shard: its row, the lock state, the Git state of its source, dependencies, and pending migrations

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one value: the row, its details, and `moved` when the ref is a former name |
| `--health` | Also run the shard health check and print its findings |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard migrate

```text
flint shard migrate [options] [command]
```

Shard migration management

Subcommands: `show`, `list`, `run`, `finish`, `rerun`.

##### flint shard migrate show

```text
flint shard migrate show [options] [name]
```

Show migration state — one shard or all shards if no name given

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard (omit for all-shards overview) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

##### flint shard migrate list

```text
flint shard migrate list [options] <name>
```

List all migration steps with types and status

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

##### flint shard migrate run

```text
flint shard migrate run [options] <name>
```

Execute migration — auto-chains scripts, rewrites the Mesh of a shorthand step, stops at agent/manual

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--dry-run` | Print the steps and the rewrite plan (one line per file); write nothing |

##### flint shard migrate finish

```text
flint shard migrate finish [options] <name>
```

Mark the current agent or manual step done; the file stays

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

##### flint shard migrate rerun

```text
flint shard migrate rerun [options] <name> <step>
```

Re-execute a specific step of the build (a step that the build does not have is refused)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |
| `step` | Step identifier (e.g. s2, or full step ID) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard create

```text
flint shard create [options] <name>
```

Create a local shard source (two ids) and build the shard from it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The name (the Display Name law; Proper Case is the convention), e.g. "Code Review" |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-s, --shorthand <sh>` | Shard shorthand (default: a free value derived from the name) |
| `-d, --description <text>` | Shard description on one line |
| `--setup [scope]` | Add a setup file and declare the setup scope: full, flint, or local (default: full) |
| `--no-install` | Write the source without building the shard |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard build

```text
flint shard build [options] <ref>
```

Build the shard of a record from its source: Shards/`<Name>`/ and the lock state (snapshot with the sha, or edited)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the source |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-f, --force` | Overwrite existing payload files |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, id, alias, folder, path, state \} or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard type

```text
flint shard type [options] [command]
```

Manage artifact types in editable shards

Subcommands: `add`.

##### flint shard type add

```text
flint shard type add [options] [name]
```

Create and install an artifact type

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Type name in Title Case, or Parent.Child |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--shard <sh>` | Editable owner shard |
| `--description <text>` | Semantic type description |
| `--folder <path>` | Artifact folder (default: Mesh/Types/`<Name>`) |
| `--dashboard` | Add a Dataview dashboard |
| `--note-template` | Add a note template for Templater (otmp-*) |
| `--no-install` | Write the source files without installing |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint |

#### flint shard dev

```text
flint shard dev [options] <name> <url>
```

Promote a local shard source to a remote source — git init, set remote, commit, push (the source id stays)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |
| `url` | Git remote URL (e.g., https://github.com/org/repo.git) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard clone

```text
flint shard clone [options] [spec]
```

Clone the source of a shard into Shards/(Source Remote) `<Name>` and build the shard from it (the registry says where the repository is)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `spec` | The package spec @org/name (or a bare name; owner/repo is the legacy spelling of --from-git) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--from-git <owner/repo>` | Clone this Git location; the record keeps it as git = "owner/repo" |
| `--alias <alias>` | The local name of the shard in this Flint (the key in flint.toml); the slug of the package by default |
| `--no-build` | Clone the source only; do not build the shard |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, alias, folder, spec, repo, git?, build? \} or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard fork

```text
flint shard fork [options] <source>
```

Fork a shard into a new local source with a new shard id and a new source id, and `of` on each (the origin is not changed)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `source` | The source: a package spec (@org/name[@range][#place]), an alias, an address (@`<uuid>`), a path, or owner/repo |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <name>` | The name of the fork (the Display Name law; Proper Case is the convention) |
| `--shorthand <shorthand>` | The shorthand of the fork (default: the shorthand of the source) |
| `--no-install` | Make the local source only; do not build the shard |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, id, sourceId, source, org, shard, of, name, shorthand, folder, installed \} or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard rename

```text
flint shard rename [options] [command] [ref]
```

Rename the name or the shorthand of a shard, in its local source or its remote source

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <new-name>` | The new name (the Display Name law; Proper Case is the convention), e.g. "Project Tracker" |
| `--shorthand <new-shorthand>` | The new shorthand (lowercase letters only) |
| `--json` | One JSON value: the rename result, or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} on a refusal |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard id

```text
flint shard id [options] <ref>
```

Fill the absent shard id, source id, and package name (org below spec 0.4.0) of a shard source (a shard that is only a build never mints an id)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard; or the path Shards/`<folder>` of a source |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Print what the command would do and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result: \{ ok, id, sourceId, org, shard, minted, filled, folder, notice? \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard setup

```text
flint shard setup [options] <ref>
```

Inspect or transition a shard's setup state (committed / local layers)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--complete` | Mark setup as completed |
| `--reset` | Reset setup to required |
| `--scope <scope>` | Layer to update: flint \| local \| both (default: derived from manifest.setup) |

```text
flint shard start [options] <ref>
```

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--dump` | Include required-reading file contents inline (experimental) |
| `--json` | One JSON value: the manifest text, or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} on a refusal |

Output a dynamic manifest for a shard source ((Source Remote) or (Source Local))

| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--json` | One JSON value: the manifest text, or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} on a refusal |

#### flint shard hstart

```text
flint shard hstart [options] <ref>
```

Output a headless-mode manifest for loading a shard

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--dump` | Include required-reading file contents inline (experimental) |
| `--json` | One JSON value: the manifest text, or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} on a refusal |

#### flint shard hstart-dev

```text
flint shard hstart-dev [options] <ref>
```

Output a headless-mode manifest for a shard source ((Source Remote) or (Source Local))

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--json` | One JSON value: the manifest text, or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} on a refusal |

#### flint shard push

```text
flint shard push [options] <name>
```

Commit and push a remote source to its repository

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-m, --message <msg>` | Commit message |
| `-b, --bump [level]` | Bump version in shard.yaml (patch, minor, major; default: patch) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard pull

```text
flint shard pull [options] <name>
```

Git pull a remote source

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard publish

```text
flint shard publish [options] <name> [version]
```

Deprecated alias of release: the same arguments, the same result

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the source |
| `version` | Version to release (default: read from shard.yaml) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--org <slug>` | Register under this org: a slug that this machine knows. Default: the org of the Flint. A Flint with no org does not publish, also with --org |
| `--no-register` | Tag and push only; do not register with the NUU Shard Registry |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, alias, version, tag, sha, hash, repo, registered, registry, slug, state \} or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard unpublish

```text
flint shard unpublish [options] <slug>
```

Remove a shard from the NUU Shard Registry (requires login)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `slug` | Shard slug (e.g. NUU-Cognition/notepad) |

#### flint shard published

```text
flint shard published [options]
```

List your published shards on the NUU Shard Registry

#### flint shard release

```text
flint shard release [options] <name> [version]
```

Release a version: build from a clean export of HEAD, tag v`<version>`, push, register with the NUU Shard Registry, and lock the shard as published when the registry lists the version

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the source |
| `version` | Version to release (default: read from shard.yaml) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--org <slug>` | Register under this org: a slug that this machine knows. Default: the org of the Flint. A Flint with no org does not publish, also with --org |
| `--no-register` | Tag and push only; do not register with the NUU Shard Registry |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, alias, version, tag, sha, hash, repo, registered, registry, slug, state \} or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

#### flint shard versions

```text
flint shard versions [options] <name>
```

List the versions of a shard: the registry versions, else the Git tags of its location

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, address, from, versions: [\{ tag, sha?, hash? \}], installed \} |

#### flint shard scripts

```text
flint shard scripts [options] <ref>
```

List executable scripts provided by a shard

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The package name, the alias, the shorthand, or the id of the shard |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

### flint module

```text
flint module [options] [command] [name] [command] [args...]
```

Manage the modules of this Flint (packages in Modules/), and run a command of a module

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | A module name, to run one of its commands |
| `command` | The command of the module |
| `args` | The arguments of the command |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |

Subcommands: `list`, `status`, `install`, `uninstall`, `build`, `update`, `settings`, `start`, `restart`, `stop`, `control`, `release`.

#### flint module list

```text
flint module list [options]
```

List the modules of this Flint: one row for each record of [modules]

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the list as JSON |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint module status

```text
flint module status [options] [name]
```

Show the package state and the sync plan of each module in this Flint (writes nothing)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | One module |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the status as JSON |

#### flint module install

```text
flint module install [options] [spec]
```

Install a module, or with no input make the lock and the builds match the records of [modules]

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `spec` | A package spec: @org/name[@range][#place] |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--from-git <location>` | Install from a Git location: owner/repo[#ref] |
| `--from-path <dir>` | Install from a folder on this machine (a source runs its build command first) |
| `--name <name>` | The record name in [modules] (default: the slug of the module name) |
| `--dry-run` | With no input: show what would change; write nothing |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module uninstall

```text
flint module uninstall [options] <name>
```

Remove a module: its build, its lock record, and its record. The settings file and the data folder stay

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module build

```text
flint module build [options] <ref>
```

Build a module from its source in this Flint (Modules/(Source Local|Remote) `<Name>`), and record from = "source"

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | The module name, or the source folder name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <name>` | The record name in [modules] (default: the record of the source, else its slug) |
| `--broadcast` | Then update the module in each Flint of this machine that installs it from this Flint |
| `--dry-run` | With --broadcast: show the Flints that would update; write nothing |
| `--only <flint...>` | With --broadcast: update these Flints only |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module update

```text
flint module update [options] [name]
```

Move each module to the newest version inside the range of its record

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | One module |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Show the updates; write nothing |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module settings

```text
flint module settings [options] <name> <action> <key> [value]
```

Read or write one key of the settings file of a module (Modules/`<Name>`.settings.toml); a write keeps every comment

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name |
| `action` | get or set |
| `key` | A dotted key, for example machine or schedules.tick.machine |
| `value` | With set: one TOML value. A string needs quotes: '"check-box"' |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--create` | With set: make the table of a nested key when the file does not have it |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module start

```text
flint module start [options] <name>
```

Start a live module under the Flint server of this Flint (starts the server when none runs)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module restart

```text
flint module restart [options] <name>
```

Stop the process of a live module, if one runs, and start a new one (starts the server when none runs)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module stop

```text
flint module stop [options] <name>
```

Stop a live module: SIGTERM, then SIGKILL after 5 s. It starts again with flint module start or the next start of the Flint server

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module control

```text
flint module control [options] <name> [action]
```

Call an action of the controller of a running live module, or with no action list its actions

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name |
| `action` | The action of the controller |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--data <json>` | The input of the action: one JSON object (default \{\}) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint module release

```text
flint module release [options] <name> [version]
```

Release a version of a module source: run its build step, check that the build output is committed, tag v`<version>`, push, register the module with the NUU Shard Registry, and lock it as published when the registry lists the version

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The module name, or the source folder name |
| `version` | Version to release (default: read from module.yaml) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--org <slug>` | Register under this org: a slug that this machine knows. Default: the org of the Flint. A Flint with no org does not publish, also with --org |
| `--no-register` | Tag and push only; do not register with the NUU Shard Registry |
| `--json` | One JSON value: \{ ok, alias, version, tag, sha, hash, repo, registered, registry, slug, state \} or \{ ok: false, code, reason, next \} |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint |

### flint config

```text
flint config [options] [command]
```

The Flint CLI config file (~/.nuucognition/flint-dev/config.toml). name, machine-name, and org route to the NUU config

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

Subcommands: `open`, `get`, `set`, `name`, `machine-name`, `org`.

#### flint config open

```text
flint config open [options]
```

Open the Flint CLI config file (~/.nuucognition/flint-dev/config.toml) in your editor

#### flint config get

```text
flint config get [options] <key>
```

Get a value of the Flint CLI config file (~/.nuucognition/flint-dev/config.toml)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `key` | Config key (dot notation, e.g. orbit.devOrigins). obsidian prints on or off; obsidian-trust prints auto or ask; update-check prints on or off |

#### flint config set

```text
flint config set [options] <key> <value>
```

Set a value in the Flint CLI config file (~/.nuucognition/flint-dev/config.toml)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `key` | Config key (dot notation, e.g. orbit.devOrigins): a dotted key is a nested table. obsidian takes on or off; obsidian-trust takes auto or ask; update-check takes on or off |
| `value` | Value to set (JSON for arrays/objects) |

#### flint config name

```text
flint config name [options] [name]
```

Your Name, the author of your notes. Routes to the NUU config (~/.nuucognition/config.toml)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Name to store (e.g. "Nathan Luo") |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--unset` | Unset the stored name |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### flint config machine-name

```text
flint config machine-name [options] [name]
```

The branch prefix of this machine (machine-name). Routes to the NUU config (~/.nuucognition/config.toml)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The branch prefix to store |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--unset` | Unset the branch prefix; the hostname is the default |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### flint config org

```text
flint config org [options] [command]
```

The org of this machine: new Flints get it. Routes to the NUU config (~/.nuucognition/config.toml)

Subcommands: `show`, `set`, `unset`, `map`.

##### flint config org show

```text
flint config org show [options]
```

Show the org of this machine

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

##### flint config org set

```text
flint config org set [options] <slug>
```

Set the org of this machine. The id comes from --id, the enrollment of this machine, or a Flint of this machine with that org

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `slug` | The org slug, for example nuucognition |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--id <uuid>` | The id of the org. The admin of the org gives it. Not needed when this machine knows the org |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

##### flint config org unset

```text
flint config org unset [options]
```

Remove the org of this machine: new Flints are local (no org). The Flints, the enrollment, and the account do not change

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

##### flint config org map

```text
flint config org map [options] <slug> <github-owner>
```

Map a GitHub owner to an org: one [orgs.`<slug>`] table in the NUU config (~/.nuucognition/config.toml). flint migrate run reads it for the Git records that the registry does not know

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `slug` | The org slug, for example nuu-cognition |
| `github-owner` | The GitHub user or organization whose repositories hold the shards of the org |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

### flint obsidian

```text
flint obsidian [options] [command]
```

Manage the Obsidian payload of this Flint

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | The Flint (default: the Flint of this folder) |

Subcommands: `status`, `update`, `repair`, `reload`, `backups`, `profiles`, `applied`, `vaults`.

#### flint obsidian status

```text
flint obsidian status [options]
```

Show the payload (pinned, installed, available, pending), the vault, the plugin, and the next command

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the status model as JSON |
| `--check` | Also probe each Obsidian manager for an old plugin, and exit 2 when an error or a warning is present |
| `--tinderbox` | Print one row for each member of the Tinderbox |

#### flint obsidian update

```text
flint obsidian update [options]
```

Pin the recommended release and install it; keep every personal setting

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--to <release>` | Install this release (a version such as 0.7.1) |
| `--allow-downgrade` | With --to: allow a release that is older than the pin |
| `--replace` | Replace any object at a release path, after a backup |
| `--tinderbox` | Update each member of the Tinderbox, and print one row for each |
| `--only <names...>` | With --tinderbox: update only these members |
| `--dry-run` | Print the plan and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint obsidian repair

```text
flint obsidian repair [options]
```

Restore the release files of the pinned release; --replace also replaces any object at a release path after a backup

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--replace` | Replace every changed release file and every object at a release path, after a backup |
| `--reset-settings` | Replace each settings file with its initial settings, after a backup |
| `--file <path>` | With --reset-settings: reset only this settings file |
| `--yes` | On an open vault: reload Obsidian after the repair also when another terminal tab runs a process |
| `--dry-run` | Print the plan and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint obsidian reload

```text
flint obsidian reload [options]
```

Reload Obsidian for this Flint; list the terminal tabs that stop

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--yes` | Reload also when a terminal tab runs a process |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

#### flint obsidian backups

```text
flint obsidian backups [options] [command]
```

List, show, restore, and prune the Obsidian backups

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `restore`, `prune`.

##### flint obsidian backups list

```text
flint obsidian backups list [options]
```

List the backups, newest first

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--scope <scope>` | flint, registry, deleted, or all (choices: "flint", "registry", "deleted", "all", default: "all") |
| `--json` | Print the backups as JSON |

##### flint obsidian backups show

```text
flint obsidian backups show [options] <id>
```

Show every field of one backup and each saved path

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | The backup id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print backup.json |

##### flint obsidian backups restore

```text
flint obsidian backups restore [options] <id>
```

Restore a backup: in full, one path with --file, or into an empty folder with --to

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | The backup id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--file <path>` | Restore only this path |
| `--to <empty-dir>` | Copy the saved files into an empty folder; change nothing in the Flint |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

##### flint obsidian backups prune

```text
flint obsidian backups prune [options]
```

Delete old backups: a preview without --yes. The newest backup of each scope stays

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--keep <n>` | Keep the newest n backups of each scope |
| `--older-than <duration>` | Delete the backups older than a duration: `<n>`h, `<n>`d, or `<n>`w |
| `--scope <scope>` | flint, registry, deleted, or all (choices: "flint", "registry", "deleted", "all", default: "flint") |
| `--include-legacy` | Also delete legacy imports |
| `--yes` | Delete the backups of the preview |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |

### flint migrate

```text
flint migrate [options] [command]
```

Run and inspect the migrations of this Flint

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |
| `--agentic <version>` | Start one agent session that parks the sessions, copies every Flint, and migrates every Flint of this machine (the one-time sweep: 070) |
| `--print` | With --agentic: print the prompt of the session; write nothing and start nothing |
| `--json` | With --agentic: print the plan as one JSON value; start nothing |

Subcommands: `list`, `run`, `shared`, `local`, `rollback`, `finish`, `rerun`, `machine`.

#### flint migrate list

```text
flint migrate list [options]
```

List migration steps

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `-a, --all` | Show all steps including completed (useful for finding rerun targets) |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint migrate run

```text
flint migrate run [options]
```

Run pending migration steps

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--scope <scope>` | Filter by scope: shared \| local |
| `--to <version>` | Stop at this version |
| `--dry-run` | Inspect and plan without writing files |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `--resume <run>` | Resume an unfinished migration run |
| `--retire-legacy-local` | Move old .flint state that no step moves into .flint/legacy/`<run>`/ |

#### flint migrate shared

```text
flint migrate shared [options]
```

Run pending shared migrations

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--scope <scope>` | Filter by scope: shared \| local |
| `--to <version>` | Stop at this version |
| `--dry-run` | Inspect and plan without writing files |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `--resume <run>` | Resume an unfinished migration run |
| `--retire-legacy-local` | Move old .flint state that no step moves into .flint/legacy/`<run>`/ |

#### flint migrate local

```text
flint migrate local [options]
```

Run pending local migrations

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--scope <scope>` | Filter by scope: shared \| local |
| `--to <version>` | Stop at this version |
| `--dry-run` | Inspect and plan without writing files |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `--resume <run>` | Resume an unfinished migration run |
| `--retire-legacy-local` | Move old .flint state that no step moves into .flint/legacy/`<run>`/ |

#### flint migrate rollback

```text
flint migrate rollback [options] [run]
```

Restore a migration backup if its files have no later edits

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `run` | Migration run ID (default: latest) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |

#### flint migrate finish

```text
flint migrate finish [options]
```

Verify and complete the exact started agent or manual step

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint migrate rerun

```text
flint migrate rerun [options] <step-id>
```

Verify and run a script step through a migration transaction

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `step-id` | Step ID |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |

#### flint migrate machine

```text
flint migrate machine [options]
```

Survey every Flint of this machine before a machine migration (reads only)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON result |
| `--only <names...>` | Only these Flints (name or path) |
| `--to <version>` | The target version (default: the version of this CLI) |
| `--probe` | Read each remote once (git ls-remote, 10 second timeout) |
| `--agentic <version>` | Start the sweep session of this version: park the sessions, copy every Flint, and migrate every Flint (the same as flint migrate --agentic) |
| `--print` | With --agentic: print the prompt of the session; write nothing and start nothing |

### flint orbh

```text
flint orbh [options] [command]
```

Orbh meta-harness agent management

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

Subcommands: `launch`, `ping`, `step`, `i`, `tui`, `v`, `resume`, `continue`, `request`, `wait`, `attach`, `detach`, `session`, `update`, `rooms`, `improve`, `approval`, `respond`, `result`, `requests`, `message`, `humanchannel`, `artifact`, `end`, `kill`, `close`, `park`, `discard`, `focus`, `list`, `active`, `timeline`, `inspect`, `peek`, `stats`, `watch`, `context`, `scope`, `space`, `station`, `cron`, `move-spool`, `save`, `restore`, `import`, `runtimes`, `profiles`, `auth`, `claude`, `heal`, `verify`, `verify-sessions`, `rebuild-session-snapshots`, `rebuild`, `orchestrator`, `server`, `archive`, `reconcile`, `interrupt`, `page`, `job`, `usage`, `room`, `fleet`, `actor`, `stream`, `agent`, `worker`, `procedure`, `workflow`, `compact`, `command-action`, `switch`, `copy-spools`, `store`, `fork`.

#### flint orbh launch

```text
flint orbh launch [options] [target] [prompt]
```

Launch a new headless agent session

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `target` | Harness runtime or runtime/name (agy, claude, codex, cursor, droid, grok, kimi, muse, opencode). Omit it to use the default target list; the first argument is then the prompt |
| `prompt` | Prompt for the agent (or pass --prompt-file) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--prompt-file <path>` | Read the prompt from a UTF-8 file instead of the argument. Keeps a typed launch command short when the prompt is long. |
| `--continues <sessionId>` | Link as continuation of another session |
| `--max-turns <n>` | Maximum number of agent turns |
| `--budget <usd>` | Maximum budget in USD (where supported by the harness) |
| `--model <model>` | Model override |
| `--account <name>` | Harness account name, or 'none' to bypass the runtime default |
| `--allow-switch` | Let the account autoswitch move an explicit --account to a good fallback account when it is limited |
| `--allow-manual-only` | Let --account name a manual-only account (auth manual-only) |
| `--title <title>` | Pre-set session title (skips agent self-registration) |
| `--description <description>` | Pre-set session description |
| `--application <name>` | Tag the session with the launching application (also read from ORBH_APPLICATION); tagged sessions are hidden from list, active, and the cockpit by default |
| `--fleet <handle>` | Make the new session a member of this open fleet (stamp via launch, one roster event) |
| `--declare-fleet <handle>` | Declare a new fleet with the new session as its root (needs --charter-file) |
| `--charter-file <path>` | The charter of the fleet that --declare-fleet declares: the goal, the boundary, and the done condition |
| `--fleet-name <name>` | The display name of the fleet that --declare-fleet declares (default: the handle in Title Case) |
| `--force` | Bypass the live-run guard (may create concurrent runtimes) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh ping

```text
flint orbh ping [options] [targetOrPrompt] [prompt]
```

Run one unattended inference turn and print its returned string

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `targetOrPrompt` | Harness runtime or runtime/name (agy, claude, codex, cursor, droid, grok, kimi, muse, opencode); with --continue, the complete prompt |
| `prompt` | Complete prompt for a fresh ping |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-c, --continue <predecessorId>` | Continue a returned ping in a new spool |
| `--account <name>` | Harness account name, or 'none' to bypass the runtime default |
| `--application <name>` | Tag the session with the launching application (also read from ORBH_APPLICATION); tagged sessions are hidden from list, active, and the cockpit by default |
| `--json` | Print \{"sessionId","result"\} so the returned ping can be chained with --continue |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh step

```text
flint orbh step [options] [command]
```

Step mode: run the steps a Flint declares for itself ([orbh.step] in flint.toml), one headless turn per step.

Subcommands: `start`, `list`, `show`.

##### flint orbh step start

```text
flint orbh step start [options]
```

Take one step (or N with --auto) on the Flint's path, printing each returned step report as it lands.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--auto <n>` | Run up to N steps in sequence; stops early on the contract's stop conditions |
| `--on <name>` | Path name to run on (default: the contract's path); replaces \{path\} in the prompt |
| `--entry <text>` | Designate a queue entry: appended to the prompt as "Designated queue entry: `<text>`" |
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | Override the contract's launch target |
| `--account <name>` | Override the contract's harness account |
| `--dry-run` | Print the resolved contract and the request argv without launching |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |


The paths in this Flint with their counters and the last step's index line.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

##### flint orbh step show

```text
flint orbh step show [options] [path]
```

The last N step reports of a path (default: the contract's path, last 1).

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `path` | Path name, e.g. Main |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--last <n>` | How many reports, newest first (default: "1") |
| `--json` | Machine-readable output |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |

#### flint orbh i

```text
flint orbh i|interactive [options] [target] [prompt]
```

Launch an interactive agent TUI with Orbh session tracking; --continue follows a completed compaction to its successor

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `target` | Harness runtime or runtime/name (agy, claude, codex, cursor, droid, grok, kimi, muse, opencode). Omit it to use the default target list; the first argument is then the prompt |
| `prompt` | Optional initial prompt (or pass --prompt-file) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dev-channels` | Load development MCP channels (e.g. orbh) |
| `--prompt-file <path>` | Read the prompt from a UTF-8 file instead of the argument. Keeps a typed launch command short when the prompt is long. |
| `-c, --continue <sessionId>` | Continue a previous Orbh session (completed compaction predecessors resolve to the successor) |
| `--continues <sessionId>` | Link as continuation of another session (new session) |
| `--session-id <uuid>` | Pre-allocate the new session id (full UUID). Lets an external launcher know the id before the session registers. |
| `--model <model>` | Model override |
| `--account <name>` | Harness account name, or 'none' to bypass the runtime default |
| `--no-detach` | Run in legacy foreground handoff mode instead of attaching to a detachable daemon |
| `--detached` | Start the detachable daemon and return at once: print the session id and the attach command, attach nothing (detached launch) |
| `--plain` | Use the explicit raw transport mode instead of the App Layer |
| `--detachable` | Run as a detachable daemon (deprecated; detachable is the TTY default — use --no-detach to opt out) |
| `--close-after <seconds>` | Bounded run: close the runtime + TTY after N seconds (testing/acceptance) |
| `--force` | Bypass the live-run guard when continuing (may create concurrent runtimes) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh tui

```text
flint orbh tui [options]
```

Open the full-screen Orbh cockpit

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--view <sessions\|active\|usage\|agents\|updates\|approvals\|discord\|steps>` | Initial cockpit view (default: "sessions") |
| `--renderer <ink\|opentui>` | Cockpit renderer (default: opentui) |
| `--mode <full\|vertical>` | Cockpit shape: full tabbed cockpit, or the vertical split-pane multiplexer (default: "full") |

#### flint orbh v

```text
flint orbh v [options]
```

Open the vertical Orbh cockpit (tui --mode vertical)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--view <sessions\|active\|usage\|agents\|updates\|approvals\|discord\|steps>` | Initial cockpit view (default: "sessions") |
| `--renderer <ink\|opentui>` | Cockpit renderer (default: opentui) |

#### flint orbh resume

```text
flint orbh resume [options] <id> [prompt]
```

Resume a headless session with a prompt; live detachable managers attach instead, and verified runtime-declared returned processes are reclaimed

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial) |
| `prompt` | Prompt for the resumed headless turn (required unless rerouting to a live detachable manager) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--account <name>` | Account for a session that has no stored account |
| `--force` | Bypass the live-run guard (may create concurrent runtimes; prefer --steal) |
| `--steal` | Terminate the live non-detachable run before resuming; detachable interactive sessions attach instead (attach takeover is automatic) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh continue

```text
flint orbh continue|c [options]
```

Pick a session interactively and continue it on the profile it started with

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-S, --status <status>` | Filter by session workState (e.g. working, finished) |
| `-r, --runtime <runtime>` | Filter by runtime (e.g. claude, codex) |
| `-q, --search <text>` | Pre-filter title, description, and prompt (case-insensitive). The picker also has an interactive `/` search. |
| `--force` | Bypass the live-run guard (may create concurrent runtimes) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh request

```text
flint orbh request [options] [target] [text...]
```

Launch a request turn and wait for its correlated result, or post a deferred question to an existing session

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `target` | Runtime/name target, session ID, or prompt (with --runtime, or with no target: the default target list applies) |
| `text` | Prompt text or deferred question |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--runtime <runtime>` | Harness runtime or runtime/name target (alternative to positional runtime) |
| `--stream` | Stream the live transcript while waiting |
| `--stream-attempt <json>` | Reserved stream binding (used by step start) |
| `-c, --continue <sessionId>` | Resume a previous session and wait for the result of the turn this command initiates |
| `--continues <sessionId>` | Link as continuation of another session (new session) |
| `--max-turns <n>` | Maximum number of agent turns |
| `--budget <usd>` | Maximum budget in USD (where supported by the harness) |
| `--model <model>` | Model override |
| `--account <name>` | Harness account name, or 'none' to bypass the runtime default |
| `--allow-switch` | Let the account autoswitch move an explicit --account to a good fallback account when it is limited |
| `--allow-manual-only` | Let --account name a manual-only account (auth manual-only) |
| `--title <title>` | Pre-set session title |
| `--description <description>` | Pre-set session description |
| `--application <name>` | Tag the session with the launching application (also read from ORBH_APPLICATION); tagged sessions are hidden from list, active, and the cockpit by default |
| `--timeout <seconds>` | Maximum time to wait before detaching |
| `--output-schema <schema>` | JSON Schema (a file path, or inline JSON) that each return of the new session must match; a matching return is stored as result.structured |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON: the structured value when the return matched an output schema, else the text as a JSON string (implies -q) |
| `-q, --quiet` | Suppress spinner and status output, print only the raw result (for agent use) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

Wait for the correlated results of one or more currently anchored turns

| Argument | Description |

| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |

#### flint orbh attach

```text
flint orbh attach [options] [id]
```

Attach to a detachable interactive session, following completed compaction predecessors to their successor; browse detached sessions when no id is given. Modern clients cut over automatically during compaction; failed or legacy cutover prints `flint orbh attach <successor>` for manual recovery. Detach with Ctrl-\ or `orbh detach <id>`.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--print` | Print the detached-session table instead of opening the picker (no id only) |
| `--json` | Print the detached-session listing as JSON (no id only) |
| `--plain` | Use the explicit raw transport mode instead of the App Layer |
| `--steal` | Deprecated no-op: attach always takes over automatically; the displaced client is notified |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh detach

```text
flint orbh detach [options] [id]
```

Detach the active client of a detachable interactive session from another terminal — the agent keeps running. Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID inside a harness.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial). Optional when ORBH_SESSION_ID is set. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh session

```text
flint orbh session [options] <id> [action] [value...]
```

Agent-facing session lifecycle commands. Compaction moved to `flint orbh compact start` / `handoff` / `finish` (agent-authored handoff, in-process only). When ORBH_SESSION_ID is set, `<id>` may be omitted for self-targeting actions (register, return, set, get, ask).

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial), or the action verb when ORBH_SESSION_ID is set |
| `action` | Action: register, return, set, get, ask, note — or use "await" for event subscription |
| `value` | Arguments for the selected action |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--timeout <seconds>` | Timeout in seconds (default: "3600") |
| `-f, --filter <key=value>` | Filter payloads by field value (for await); repeat to add more (default: []) |
| `--list` | List available event types (for await) |
| `--host <host>` | Server host override (for await) |
| `--port <port>` | Server port override (for await) |
| `--orb-store-dir <path>` | Override the Orb store directory (.orb) used to locate the server (for await) |
| `--config <path>` | Path to orbh.toml (for await) |
| `--finish` | Return this turn and finish the session (default) |
| `--await` | Return this turn and leave the session awaiting follow-up |
| `--until-group <group>` | With return --await: resume when every job in this group is terminal |
| `--barrier-timeout <seconds>` | With return --await --until-group: fail still-running group jobs after this many seconds |
| `--wake-at <when>` | With return --await: wake on a schedule. ISO 8601 datetime (2026-07-26T14:02) or +duration (+45m, +2h, +1h30m, +1d) |
| `--wide` | Render full-width list output without narrow stacking |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

```text
Compaction has moved to its own in-process lifecycle:
  flint orbh compact start      Print the handoff contract + the path to write it to; hold the pager, show [Compacting...].
  flint orbh compact handoff    TURN-ENDING: validate the handoff, end this context, and relaunch the same session; use --into runtime/profile to change runtime or profile.
  flint orbh compact finish     In the fresh context, confirm the handoff was read and clear the compaction marker.
  flint orbh compact abort    Release the pager hold if you decide not to compact.
```

#### flint orbh update

```text
flint orbh update [options] [command] <text...>
```

Durable, non-blocking operator updates from inside a session

```text

```

List a session's updates (default: your own), --all for every session, or --fleet `<handle>` for the members of one fleet

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--all` | Aggregate updates across every session in this flint |
| `--fleet [handle]` | Only the members of the declared fleet `<handle>` (a bare --fleet is the deprecated name of --all) |
| `--limit <n>` | Most recent entries to show (default 20) |
| `--json` | Emit JSON for tooling |


Mirror pending updates to Discord now, bypassing the burst window

|---|---|

