| `--no-kill` | Do not SIGTERM the recorded live process when no tab can be closed |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh kill

```text
flint orbh kill [options] [id]
```

Kill a running session process and its process group. Refuses while descendant sessions still run, unless --force

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID inside a harness |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--force` | Kill even when descendant sessions still run; they keep running without a parent |
| `--cascade` | After the kill, abandon each awaiting descendant session (reason parent-killed), so no later wake resumes it |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh close

```text
flint orbh close [options] [id]
```

Close this session — record closed retention, terminate the harness, then close the bound Obsidian terminal tab. Pass --no-leaf to leave the tab open at a shell prompt. Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID when run from inside an Orbh harness.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial). Optional when ORBH_SESSION_ID is set. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-leaf` | Leave the bound Obsidian terminal tab open — the pane returns to a shell prompt |
| `--obsidian` | Deprecated: closing the bound tab is now the default. Accepted so existing callers keep working |
| `--force` | Close even when descendant sessions still run; they keep running without a parent |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh park

```text
flint orbh park [options] [id]
```

Await further activity for this session (the legacy `park` spelling) — record awaiting retention, pin it to `flint orbh c`, ensure the Orbh orchestrator wake sweep, terminate the current harness turn, then close the bound Obsidian terminal tab. Pass --no-leaf to leave the tab open at a shell prompt. With --until-group, await the all-terminal job-group barrier. Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID when run from inside an Orbh harness.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial). Optional when ORBH_SESSION_ID is set. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--until-group <group>` | Await until every job in this group reaches a terminal state (done OR failed), then resume with the results |
| `--barrier-timeout <seconds>` | With --until-group: force-resolve the barrier after this many seconds (still-running group jobs are marked failed) so the session can never be stranded |
| `--force` | Allow parking a confirmed-live run when no interactive manager can terminate it, or another session whose descendant sessions still run |
| `--no-leaf` | Leave the bound Obsidian terminal tab open — the pane returns to a shell prompt |
| `--obsidian` | Deprecated: closing the bound tab is now the default. Accepted so existing callers keep working |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh discard

```text
flint orbh discard [options] [id]
```

Discard this session — tombstone its entry (event-sourced; hidden from `flint orbh list`), terminate the harness, then close the bound Obsidian terminal tab. Pass --no-leaf to leave the tab open at a shell prompt. No confirmation. Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID when run from inside an Orbh harness.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial). Optional when ORBH_SESSION_ID is set. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-leaf` | Leave the bound Obsidian terminal tab open — the pane returns to a shell prompt |
| `--obsidian` | Deprecated: closing the bound tab is now the default. Accepted so existing callers keep working |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh list

```text
flint orbh list [options]
```

Open the Sessions cockpit in a TTY; use --print for the static table or --json for machine-readable output

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-a, --all` | Include sessions hidden from the default work queue: discarded sessions and application-tagged sessions, which print under a Special section (--print/--json only; the cockpit toggles them with x) |
| `--application <name>` | Only sessions tagged with this application (implies --all for them) |
| `-s, --stats` | Print token/turn stats (prefers persisted run context; otherwise reads transcripts) |
| `-S, --status <status>` | Filter by session workState (e.g. working, finished) |
| `-r, --runtime <runtime>` | Filter by runtime (e.g. claude, codex) |
| `-q, --search <text>` | Search the id prefix, title, description, and prompt (case-insensitive) |
| `--json` | Print machine-readable JSON (the default when invoked from inside an Orbh session) |
| `--print` | Print the static session table instead of opening the cockpit |
| `--wide` | Print the static table full-width without narrow stacking |
| `--subagents` | Flatten subagent sessions into top-level rows instead of rendering dispatch trees |
| `--all-scopes` | List sessions machine-wide — every registered Flint store plus ~/.orb — grouped by scope (static table output) |
| `--detached` | Only detached-interactive sessions |
| `--fleet <handle>` | Only the members of the declared fleet `<handle>`: the root, the sessions that carry its stamp, and its explicit members |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

|---|---|

| `-q, --search <text>` | Search the id prefix, title, description, and prompt (case-insensitive) |
| `--json` | Print machine-readable JSON (the default when invoked from inside an Orbh session) |
| `--print` | Print the static session tree instead of opening the cockpit |
| `--wide` | Print the static tree full-width without narrow stacking |
| `--subagents` | Flatten subagent sessions into top-level rows instead of rendering dispatch trees |
| `--detached` | Only detached-interactive sessions |

| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint orbh timeline

```text
flint orbh timeline [options] [root-session-id]
```

Reconstruct a read-only chronological timeline for a session tree, a declared fleet, or a room; it reads archived sessions too

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `root-session-id` | Root session ID (full or partial), or fleet:`<handle>` for the members of a declared fleet and their trees; omit with --room |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--room <room>` | Use all participants in a room as timeline roots |
| `--since <time>` | Include events at or after an ISO time or relative age (for example 2h or 3d) |
| `--until <time>` | Include events at or before an ISO time or relative age |
| `--depth <n>` | Maximum dispatch-tree depth |
| `--classes <classes>` | Comma-separated event classes (runs, rooms, jobs, interface, delivery, messages, requests, sessions, dispatches, continuations, notes) |
| `--json` | Print the stable machine-readable report (the default inside an Orbh session) |
| `--markdown` | Print the Markdown narrative even inside an Orbh session |
| `--full` | Disable high-volume event collapsing |
| `--no-collapse` | Disable high-volume event collapsing |
| `--collapse-threshold <n>` | Collapse consecutive same-session/class spans longer than this (default: "8") |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh scope

```text
flint orbh scope [options]
```

Pre-flight probe: print which Orb store the next command will resolve to, and why. Read-only, takes no session id, creates nothing. With --expect it exits non-zero on a mismatch, so a brief can require it before any mutating verb.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--expect <dir>` | Assert the resolved store root equals this path; exit 1 if it does not |
| `--json` | Machine-readable output |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |

#### flint orbh save

```text
flint orbh save [options] [id]
```

Save a session's native transcript files into a portable bundle

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Orbh session ID (full or partial; defaults to $ORBH_SESSION_ID) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-o, --out <dir>` | Bundle output directory (default: `<cwd>`/.orbh/bundles/`<runtime>`-`<native-id>`) |
| `--runtime <runtime>` | Treat `<id>` as a NATIVE session id of this runtime, bypassing the orbh session store |
| `--cwd <dir>` | Working directory the session ran in (overrides the recorded one) |
| `--no-record` | Write nothing to the session store: no blob, no event, no path of the bundle directory. The session stays byte for byte the same (a network transfer). |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh restore

```text
flint orbh restore [options] <bundleDir>
```

Restore a saved session bundle into this machine's native harness storage

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `bundleDir` | Path to a bundle directory (contains bundle.json) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--cwd <dir>` | Destination working directory for the restored session (default: context root) |
| `--force` | Overwrite native session files that already exist |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh import

```text
flint orbh import [options] <bundleDir>
```

Import a saved session bundle as a full Orbh session on this machine (same session id)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `bundleDir` | Path to a bundle directory (contains bundle.json with an orb/ spool bundle) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--cwd <dir>` | Working directory the restored native files are keyed to (default: the bundle's recorded cwd when it exists here, else the context root) |
| `--account <name>` | THIS-machine account the imported session runs under (default: same-name match against the session's pinned account) |
| `--force` | Replace an existing spool with the same id, and overwrite differing native files; refuses a session with a running run or a live manager (end the run first) |
| `--servers-confirmed <note>` | Below writer epoch 2, confirm with a note that no process outside the registries (Flint servers, Orbh servers, collectors) of an older build runs; a --force import over an existing spool needs it |
| `--check` | Write nothing: print the session id, whether it exists here, and every native file the import would write with its current existence |
| `--json` | With --check: print the check as JSON |
| `--no-record-paths` | Record no path of this machine (the bundle directory, the store root) in the transfer record of the imported session (a network transfer) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh runtimes

```text
flint orbh runtimes [options]
```

List discovered Orbh runtimes and their executable details

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint orbh profiles

```text
flint orbh profiles [options] [command] [target]
```

Inspect and sync Orbh runtime profiles

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `target` | Optional runtime or runtime/name target |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

Subcommands: `switch`, `default`, `update`, `push`.

##### flint orbh profiles switch

```text
flint orbh profiles switch [options] <target>
```

Move THIS session onto another profile of the SAME runtime, keeping its conversation. The run is closed at a turn boundary and the same native context resumes under the new model/effort. An interactive pane swaps its child in place; a headless session immediately relaunches a same-spool continuation. Cross-runtime is not possible here — use `compact handoff --into` for that.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `target` | Destination profile registry key (`<runtime>`/`<profile>`, same runtime as this session) |

##### flint orbh profiles default

```text
flint orbh profiles default [options] [command]
```

Show the default target list: the layer of each entry, the state of each target, and the target that a launch with no target selects

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON envelope on stdout |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

Subcommands: `set`, `unset`.

###### flint orbh profiles default set

```text
flint orbh profiles default set [options] <targets...>
```

Write the machine layer of the default target list (the key defaultTargets in the Orbh machine settings file); --flint writes the Flint layer

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `targets` | One or more runtime/profile targets, in order (for example claude/o55h codex/a6h) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--flint` | Act on the Flint layer ([orbh] default in the flint.toml of this Flint) instead of the machine layer |
| `--json` | Print one JSON envelope on stdout |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

###### flint orbh profiles default unset

```text
flint orbh profiles default unset [options]
```

Remove the machine layer of the default target list; --flint removes the Flint layer. The output names the layer that applies then

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--flint` | Act on the Flint layer ([orbh] default in the flint.toml of this Flint) instead of the machine layer |
| `--json` | Print one JSON envelope on stdout |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh profiles update

```text
flint orbh profiles update [options]
```

Pull the latest shared default profiles into ~/.nuucognition/orbh/default.json (leaves your profiles.json untouched)

##### flint orbh profiles push

```text
flint orbh profiles push [options]
```

Publish your local default.json to the shared profiles repo (maintainers)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-m, --message <message>` | Commit message |

#### flint orbh auth

```text
flint orbh auth [options] [command]
```

Manage isolated harness login accounts

Subcommands: `migrate`, `fallback`, `add`, `account`, `list`, `default`, `rename`, `remove`, `deactivate`, `reactivate`, `manual-only`, `usage`, `refresh`, `ccusage`.

##### flint orbh auth migrate

```text
flint orbh auth migrate [options] [account]
```

Move a session to another account of the same runtime (default: THIS session; pass --session for another): its conversation files are MOVED to the new account home and the durable account is flipped. An interactive pane resumes the same context there; a headless session has a live turn KILLED at a run boundary (that turn is lost, the conversation is not) and comes up on the new account at its next wake. Nothing but the session travels — memory, config, and credentials stay with the old account. With --release-claim (no account): clear a migration claim that a failed release or a stuck migration left, after a check that no migration can still change files; the files are settled first.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `account` | Destination account name (same runtime as the session); not used with --release-claim |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--session <id>` | Session to migrate (full or partial ID); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID, the session that runs this command |
| `--force` | Migrate even when the destination account has no usable credentials |
| `--release-claim` | Clear a stuck migration claim of the session; refuses while its migration can still run |

##### flint orbh auth fallback

```text
flint orbh auth fallback [options] [command]
```

The account autoswitch: an ordered fallback list of accounts for each runtime. A new headless launch whose account is in the list and limited (a usage-limit marker, or a fresh usage snapshot window at or above the switch percent) uses the first good account of the list, and prints one notice. Only with the separate opt-in `settings --auto-migrate on` (default off), the orchestrator also moves a headless session whose turn stopped on a provider limit to a good account of the list, between turns; that move uses the migration primitive of auth migrate, which still has open races. With no list, Orbh never switches. Orbh never chooses an account outside the list, and never switches an interactive launch or session.

Subcommands: `show`, `set`, `unset`, `check`, `settings`.

###### flint orbh auth fallback show

```text
flint orbh auth fallback show [options] [runtime]
```

Show the fallback lists, the switch percent, the refresh interval, and the state of each listed account. Writes nothing

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Show only this runtime |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the config and the account states as JSON |

###### flint orbh auth fallback set

```text
flint orbh auth fallback set [options] <runtime> <accounts...>
```

Set the ordered fallback list of one runtime. Each account must exist. The list replaces the old list of that runtime

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime, for example claude |
| `accounts` | Account names in the order to try them |

###### flint orbh auth fallback unset

```text
flint orbh auth fallback unset [options] <runtime>
```

Remove the fallback list of one runtime. Then Orbh never switches an account of that runtime

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |

###### flint orbh auth fallback check

```text
flint orbh auth fallback check [options] <runtime>
```

Dry run: show which account a new headless launch of this runtime uses now, and why. Writes nothing

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--account <name>` | The --account of the launch (default: the default account here) |
| `--allow-switch` | The --allow-switch of the launch: an explicit --account may switch |
| `--allow-manual-only` | The --allow-manual-only of the launch: --account may name a manual-only account |
| `--model <model>` | The model of the launch, for the model windows and the model markers |
| `--json` | Print the decision as JSON |

###### flint orbh auth fallback settings

```text
flint orbh auth fallback settings [options]
```

Show or set the switch percent, the usage refresh interval, and the automatic move (for all runtimes)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--percent <n>` | Switch percent, above 0 and at most 100, or 'default' (90) |
| `--auto-migrate <on\|off\|default>` | The automatic move of a headless session that stopped on a limit (default off) |
| `--refresh-minutes <n>` | Refresh interval of the usage snapshots of the listed accounts in minutes; 0 turns the refresh off; or 'default' (5) |

##### flint orbh auth add

```text
flint orbh auth add [options] <runtime> <name>
```

Create an isolated account home and run the harness login flow

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |
| `name` | Account name ([a-z0-9_-]+) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-login` | Create the account directory without opening the harness login flow |
| `--reuse-removed-name` | Reuse a removed account name. Sessions for the removed account still cannot resume. |

##### flint orbh auth account

```text
flint orbh auth account [options] <runtime> <name> [args...]
```

Run the harness CLI under an isolated account home — general arg passthrough to the runtime executable

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |
| `name` | Account name (must already exist) |
| `args` | Arguments forwarded to the harness executable (e.g. login --device-auth) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Print env and command without running the harness |

```text
Sets the runtime account-home env var (CODEX_HOME, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, …) to the
named Orbh account directory, then execs the harness binary with the remaining
args unchanged. Use this for login variants, status, logout, or any other CLI
subcommand that should touch only that account's credentials and state.

The account must already exist (create with `auth add <runtime> <name> [--no-login]`).

Examples:
  flint orbh auth account codex nathan-gmail login
  flint orbh auth account codex nathan-gmail login --device-auth
  flint orbh auth account codex nathan-gmail login status
  flint orbh auth account claude nuu /logout
  flint orbh auth account codex nathan-gmail --dry-run login --device-auth
```

##### flint orbh auth list

```text
flint orbh auth list [options] [runtime]
```

List configured harness accounts (a deactivated account is hidden unless --all)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Optional harness runtime |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--all` | List every known account, also each deactivated account, with its state |

##### flint orbh auth default

```text
flint orbh auth default [options] <runtime> [name]
```

Set or clear a default account (Flint-local when inside a Flint; machine-global otherwise)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |
| `name` | Account name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--clear` | Clear the default account at the active scope |
| `--global` | Operate on the machine-global default even inside a Flint |

##### flint orbh auth rename

```text
flint orbh auth rename [options] <runtime> <old-name> <new-name>
```

Rename an isolated account home and preserve existing session paths

| `new-name` | New account name |

```text
flint orbh auth remove [options] <runtime> <name>
```

Remove an isolated account home

| Argument | Description |

| `name` | Account name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--yes` | Remove without prompting |

##### flint orbh auth deactivate

```text
flint orbh auth deactivate [options] <runtime> <name>
```

Hide an account whose subscription lapsed: the account views hide it and a new launch refuses it. Its home, credentials, and sessions stay; resume still works

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |
| `name` | Account name |

##### flint orbh auth reactivate

```text
flint orbh auth reactivate [options] <runtime> <name>
```

Return a deactivated account to the account views and to new launches

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |
| `name` | Account name |

##### flint orbh auth manual-only

```text
flint orbh auth manual-only [options] <runtime> <name> <state>
```

Mark an account that only a person uses (a work account), or clear the mark. Orbh then sends it no usage request unless a command names it, keeps it out of every fallback list and default, never moves a session to it, and a launch names it only with --account `<name>` --allow-manual-only

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Harness runtime |
| `name` | Account name |
| `state` | on or off |

##### flint orbh auth usage

```text
flint orbh auth usage [options] [runtime] [name]
```

Report live quota / rate-limit windows per account. It reads the providers and updates the usage cache of each account

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Optional harness runtime |
| `name` | Optional account name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Emit the normalized usage rows as JSON |

##### flint orbh auth refresh

```text
flint orbh auth refresh [options] [runtime] [name]
```

Re-authenticate accounts whose access token has expired by launching one throwaway session under each

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `runtime` | Optional harness runtime |
| `name` | Optional account name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Emit the refresh outcomes as JSON |
| `--dry-run` | List the accounts that would be refreshed without launching anything |

```text
A `needs-reauth` account is almost never actually logged out — its stored access
token has simply expired, and the harness CLI refreshes it from the refresh token
the moment a session launches under that account. This command automates exactly
that: one minimal ping per stale account, then a re-check.

Success is the post-ping usage result, not the ping exit status. The refresh lands
at launch, before any inference, so a ping that fails to return its result has
still done the job.

Examples:
  flint orbh auth refresh
  flint orbh auth refresh claude
  flint orbh auth refresh claude nuucognition
  flint orbh auth refresh --dry-run
```

##### flint orbh auth ccusage

```text
flint orbh auth ccusage [options] [args...]
```

Run ccusage against Orbh account homes; add a runtime vanilla home only when that runtime has no named account

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `args` | Arguments passed to ccusage (default: daily) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Print the env and command without running ccusage |

```text
Aggregates registered account homes under ~/.nuucognition/orbh/accounts
(or ORBH_ACCOUNTS_DIR) into ccusage multi-directory env vars
(comma-separated, no spaces). A runtime with no named account also
contributes its vanilla CLI home:
  CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR  ← claude accounts, or ~/.claude (+ ~/.config/claude) if none
  CODEX_HOME         ← codex accounts, or ~/.codex if none
  GROK_HOME          ← grok accounts, or ~/.grok if none
  …                 ← any other harness accountHomeEnvVar + resolveDefaultHome

Vanilla homes are omitted for any runtime that already has a named account.
Launch with --account none still uses the vanilla home; list and usage do not
show it once named accounts exist.

Claude reports use temporary copies to support nullable iteration models.
The original transcripts stay unchanged. Temporary copies are removed after the report.

ccusage has no Muse support, so Orbh reads the Muse session logs itself
($XDG_DATA_HOME/muse, else ~/.local/share/muse). After the ccusage table of
daily, weekly, monthly, or session (and each --sections report), Orbh prints a
Muse table for the same periods; --since, --until, --timezone, --last, and --id
apply to it. With --json, the Muse rows go under the top-level key
"orbhAgents", and the ccusage keys stay unchanged. The Muse cost is
unknown (— or null). blocks, statusline, and one-agent reports get no Muse rows.
"auth ccusage muse [daily|weekly|monthly|session]" prints the Muse report alone.

Examples:
  flint orbh auth ccusage
  flint orbh auth ccusage monthly
  flint orbh auth ccusage daily --json
  flint orbh auth ccusage muse weekly
  flint orbh auth ccusage --dry-run
```




Compare every Claude home with the managed policy; exits 1 on drift


Write the managed settings into every Claude home (or one account / path)


Bring every Claude home up to date in one go: force-apply the managed settings to all homes, then show doctor

| `--dry-run` | Report what would change without writing |

| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint orbh rebuild

```text

```

Destroy this flint's derived Orb transcript events and rebuild them from the native agent transcripts. Alias: reset.

| `--json` | Print a machine-readable rebuild report |
| `--dry-run` | Preview only: print what would be wiped/rebuilt and change nothing |
| `--yes` | Actually perform the destructive wipe + rebuild (required; otherwise previews) |
| `--force` | Alias of --yes |
| `--cwd <dir>` | Override the working directory used to resolve the target flint |
| `--runtime <name>` | Only rebuild sessions of this runtime |
| `--session <id>` | Only rebuild this session |
| `--servers-confirmed <note>` | Below writer epoch 2, confirm with a note that no process outside the registries (Flint servers, Orbh servers, collectors) of an older build runs; the rebuild needs it |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

#### flint orbh orchestrator

```text
flint orbh orchestrator|orch [options] [command]
```

Inspect and control the per-machine Orbh orchestrator (supervises managers; reaps ungraceful deaths)

Subcommands: `status`, `managers`, `processes`, `launcher`, `ensure`, `stop`, `restart`, `reap`.

##### flint orbh orchestrator status

```text
flint orbh orchestrator status [options]
```

Show the per-machine orchestrator singleton + supervised manager roster

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |

##### flint orbh orchestrator managers

```text
flint orbh orchestrator managers|ls [options]
```

List managers in the orchestrator registry

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--all` | Include dead/stale registry entries (default: live only) |
| `-i, --interactive` | Pick a live manager and attach / detach / kill it (requires a TTY) |
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

##### flint orbh orchestrator processes

```text
flint orbh orchestrator processes|ps [options]
```

List the machine-wide Orbh auxiliary-process registry and report unregistered ghosts

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |
| `--stale` | Show only live processes that started before the current build |

##### flint orbh orchestrator launcher

```text
flint orbh orchestrator launcher [options] [command]
```

Inspect and declare the machine-canonical launcher that auxiliary processes are spawned through

Subcommands: `show`, `adopt`, `clear`.

###### flint orbh orchestrator launcher show

```text
flint orbh orchestrator launcher show [options]
```

Show the canonical launcher record, whether it still exists, and this process's own code identity

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |

###### flint orbh orchestrator launcher adopt

```text
flint orbh orchestrator launcher adopt [options]
```

Record the machine-canonical launcher (defaults to the launcher this invocation is running through)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--path <launcher>` | Adopt this launcher script instead of the current invocation's |
| `--kind <kind>` | Force the launcher kind (flint\|orbh) when it cannot be inferred |
| `--if-absent` | No-op when a record already exists, so an automated caller can never steal the machine record |
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |

###### flint orbh orchestrator launcher clear

```text
flint orbh orchestrator launcher clear [options]
```

Remove the canonical launcher record, restoring caller inference

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |

##### flint orbh orchestrator ensure

```text
flint orbh orchestrator ensure|start [options]
```

Ensure the per-machine orchestrator singleton is running (lazily spawns it if down)

##### flint orbh orchestrator stop

```text
flint orbh orchestrator stop [options]
```

Stop the per-machine orchestrator singleton between ticks (it is re-ensured on the next interactive launch)

##### flint orbh orchestrator restart

```text
flint orbh orchestrator restart [options]
```

Restart the per-machine orchestrator between ticks onto the build of this CLI (a hand-off: the new process loads first)

##### flint orbh orchestrator reap

```text
flint orbh orchestrator reap [options]
```

Reap dead managers now (remove stale registry entries + signal orphaned runtime groups)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output machine-readable JSON |

Standalone orbh server management

##### flint orbh server start


Start the standalone orbh HTTP/SSE server

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

| `--orb-store-dir <path>` | Override the Orb store directory (.orb) |
| `--config <path>` | Path to orbh.toml |
| `--daemon` | Detach and keep the server running in the background |


Stop the standalone orbh server daemon

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

| `--port <port>` | Server port override |
| `--orb-store-dir <path>` | Override the Orb store directory (.orb) |
| `--config <path>` | Path to orbh.toml |


Show standalone orbh server status

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--host <host>` | Server host override |
| `--port <port>` | Server port override |
| `--orb-store-dir <path>` | Override the Orb store directory (.orb) |
| `--config <path>` | Path to orbh.toml |

#### flint orbh page

#### flint orbh job

```text
flint orbh job [options] [command]
```

Run and inspect background jobs for this session (rendered by core/jobs). Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID.

Subcommands: `run`, `complete`, `list`, `show`, `result`, `wait`, `cancel`, `clear`.

##### flint orbh job run

```text
flint orbh job run [options] <command>
```

Run a shell command — or a subagent dispatch with --agent — as a background job (inherits the terminal env)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `command` | Shell command to run in the background (with --agent: the subagent prompt) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--group <g>` | Job group name — the join key for `park --until-group` |
| `--agent <target>` | Dispatch a subagent instead: `<target>` is a runtime/profile (e.g. codex/55xh) and the positional argument is its prompt |
| `--account <name>` | Harness account for --agent, or 'none' to bypass the runtime default |
| `--title <title>` | With --agent: pre-set the title of the child session |
| `--description <description>` | With --agent: pre-set the description of the child session |
| `--application <name>` | With --agent: Tag the session with the launching application (also read from ORBH_APPLICATION); tagged sessions are hidden from list, active, and the cockpit by default |
| `--output-schema <schema>` | With --agent: JSON Schema (a file path, or inline JSON) that each return of the child must match; `job result --json` prints the structured value |
| `--timeout <seconds>` | Mark the job failed (timeout) and SIGTERM its process group after this many seconds |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job complete

```text
flint orbh job complete [options] <id>
```

Record a job's terminal status (used by the background wrapper)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--exit <code>` | Exit code |
| `--output-file <path>` | Override the file holding the job's captured output (defaults to the job's recorded outputPath) |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job list

```text
flint orbh job list [options]
```

List this session's jobs (also reaps dead-wrapper and timed-out jobs)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job show

```text
flint orbh job show [options] <id>
```

Print the whole record of one job: id, kind, status, group, target, times, exit, reason, output path, and the full prompt or command

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job result

```text
flint orbh job result [options] <id>
```

Print a job's full captured output (works on running jobs too — output streams into the file)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the job as JSON: id, status, kind, childSessionId, output, and structured (present when the child's return matched an output schema) |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job wait

```text
flint orbh job wait [options] <id>
```

Block until a job reaches a terminal state, then print its full output. Exit 0 when done, 1 when failed. Reaps dead-wrapper/timed-out jobs while polling, so a wait can never hang on a job that will never report.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--poll <ms>` | Polling interval in milliseconds (default 1000) |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job cancel

```text
flint orbh job cancel [options] <id>
```

Cancel one running job, remove its process registration, and delete its retained output

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh job clear

```text
flint orbh job clear [options]
```

Remove terminal (done/failed) jobs from the Page and delete their retained outputs

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint orbh usage

```text
flint orbh usage [options] [id]
```

Show the token usage and cost of a session, its dispatch tree, or a declared fleet. Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial). Optional when ORBH_SESSION_ID is set or --fleet is given. |

|---|---|

| `--json` | Print a machine-readable object |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint orbh fleet

```text
flint orbh fleet [options] [command]
```

Declare and manage fleets: a named group of sessions that acts under one name and one charter

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

```text
A fleet is one spool in the flint Orb space of the Flint. Its handle is unique for all time.
A child of a member inherits the fleet at launch. A peer or a station joins with fleet join.
fleet close does not stop a session.
```

Subcommands: `declare`, `show`, `list`, `updates`, `join`, `leave`, `set`, `close`.

##### flint orbh fleet declare

```text
flint orbh fleet declare [options] <handle>
```

Declare a fleet. The caller session becomes the root, unless --root names a session

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `handle` | Fleet handle: 2 to 32 characters, lower case, digits, and hyphens |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <name>` | Display name (default: the handle in Title Case) |
| `--charter <text>` | The goal, the boundary, and the done condition, in 1 to 5 sentences |
| `--charter-file <path>` | Read the charter from a file |
| `--charter-ref <ref>` | Mesh path or wikilink of the source of the charter |
| `--root <sessionId>` | The root session (default: ORBH_SESSION_ID) |
| `--room <room>` | The room of the fleet |
| `--avatar <url>` | Public image URL for the Discord webhook post |
| `--max-members <n>` | Advisory member limit |
| `--budget <usd>` | Advisory budget in USD |
| `--deadline <iso\|+duration>` | Advisory deadline |
| `--no-backfill` | Do not stamp the present dispatch tree under the root |
| `--backfill-only` | Do not declare: stamp the root and each unstamped session under the root of the open fleet |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |

#### flint orbh agent

```text
flint orbh agent [options] [command]
```

Create and manage durable orb agents (orba) — named entities backed by a long-lived session

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

```text
An orb agent owns a scope of responsibility and persists across sessions.
Its identity lives in its markdown mind — `<home>/(Orb Agent) <name>.md`, whose
frontmatter carries agent/home/target/account/scope/description. Edit an agent by
editing that file; a moved mind folder self-heals on the next read. The only two
writes with verbs are the launch facts, because each does more than the edit:
`agent profile` validates the target against the profile registry, and
`agent migrate` also moves a live interactive body into the new account.
Its backing headless session is a replaceable body: `agent wake` launches one
on first use and relaunches it if the previous body ended.
Address a live agent with `flint orbh message send agent:<name> "<text>"`.
```

Subcommands: `create`, `list`, `show`, `page`, `wake`, `switch`, `pause`, `resume`, `retire`, `profile`, `migrate`, `discord`.

##### flint orbh agent create

```text
flint orbh agent create [options] <name>
```

Register a durable orb agent against the markdown mind that claims its name

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Agent name (globally unique in this Flint, case-insensitive) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--home <path>` | Mesh folder holding the agent's mind (default: discovered by scan) |
| `--json` | Print the registered agent as JSON |

```text
flint orbh agent list [options]
```

List orb agents with state, scope, backing session, and wake schedule

| `--all` | Include retired agents |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the agent as JSON |


##### flint orbh agent switch

```text
flint orbh agent switch [options] <name> <mode>
```

Switch an orb agent's live body between interactive and headless mode

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Agent name or id |
| `mode` | Target mode (interactive\|headless) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--force` | Adopt despite a live collector (interactive only) |
| `--here` | Attach this terminal to the relaunched body (interactive only) |
| `--no-launch` | Flip without starting an interactive pane (interactive only) |
| `--await` | Land awaiting instead of auto-resuming (headless only) |
| `--resume <prompt>` | Append a prompt to the restored headless turn (headless only) |

```text
flint orbh agent pause [options] <name>
```

##### flint orbh agent profile

```text
flint orbh agent profile [options] <name> [target]
```

Show or set the runtime/profile an orb agent's body launches under. Setting it writes `target:` in the agent's mind and, when the body can take it, switches that live body onto the new profile too — keeping its conversation. Cross-runtime targets only ever reach the next body.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Agent name or id |
| `target` | Target to set (`<runtime>` or `<runtime>`/`<profile>`); omit to show the current one |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--mind-only` | Write the declaration and leave the live body on its current profile |

##### flint orbh agent migrate

```text
flint orbh agent migrate [options] <name> <account>
```

Move an orb agent to another harness account of the same runtime. Writes `account:` in the agent's mind, and when its body is a live interactive pane, moves that running conversation into the new account too.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Agent name or id |
| `account` | Destination account name (same runtime as the agent) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--force` | Migrate even when the destination account has no usable credentials |
| `--mind-only` | Write the declaration and leave the live body on its current account |

##### flint orbh agent discord

```text
flint orbh agent discord [options] [command]
```

Per-agent Discord identities: bots whose messages land in the agent's session inbox

Subcommands: `setup`, `show`, `status`, `start`, `stop`, `routing`.

###### flint orbh agent discord setup

```text
flint orbh agent discord setup [options]
```

Add or update one agent's Discord identity in the machine-level config

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--agent <name>` | Orb agent name the bot belongs to |
| `--token <token>` | Discord bot token (prefer --token-stdin) |
| `--token-stdin` | Read the Discord bot token from stdin |
| `--server <id>` | Home guild id (guild messages from other servers never qualify) |
| `--channel <id>` | Default/home channel id (watched without an @-mention) |
| `--worker-channel <id>` | Channel worker threads open under (default: the home channel) |
| `--approval-forum <id>` | Forum channel this agent files its own approvals into (never created; omit = native-only) |
| `--allow <ids>` | Comma-separated allowlisted Discord user ids |
| `--updates <mode>` | Updates-mirror verbosity for this agent: 'all' (default) or 'mute' |
| `--scope <dir>` | Flint cwd owning the agent (defaults to the current Flint; must already contain .orb) |
| `--remove` | Remove this agent's entry instead of adding one |
| `--enable` | Enable the bridge |
| `--disable` | Disable the bridge (the running process exits on its own) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

```text
A worker is a headless session bound to exactly one unit and to a conversational
surface. Its parent agent spawns it with a self-contained brief; it narrates on
its own update stream, parks while it waits, converses with the human in its own
thread, and returns exactly once — the delivery result that resolves the parent's
collection. `worker spawn` does not block: the result arrives through the normal
child-result wake.

The native surface (this session spool) is always present and is never a binding.
External surfaces — a Discord thread, and later anything else — are bound
projections with inbound routing; nothing is lost when one of them dies.
```

Subcommands: `spawn`, `list`, `show`, `collect`, `say`, `kill`.

##### flint orbh worker spawn

```text
flint orbh worker spawn [options] <brief...>
```

Spawn a worker for one unit of work (non-blocking)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `brief` | The self-contained brief the worker executes |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--agent <name>` | Parent orb agent — the worker becomes its subagent |
| `--unit <title>` | Mesh title of the unit artifact this worker owns |
| `--label <label>` | Human handle (default: derived from the unit title) |
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | Launch target (default: the agent's, else the first available target of the default target list) |
| `--account <name>` | Harness account (default: the agent's) |
| `--fleet <handle>` | Make the worker a member of this open fleet when its parent is in no fleet (a member parent already gives its fleet) |
| `--output-schema <schema>` | JSON Schema (a file path, or inline JSON) that each return of the worker must match; a matching return is stored as result.structured |
| `--json` | Print the spawn outcome as JSON |

##### flint orbh worker list

```text
flint orbh worker list [options]
```

List workers with derived state, unit, and surfaces

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--agent <name>` | Only workers of this orb agent |
| `--all` | Include finished and dead workers |
| `--json` | Print workers as JSON |

##### flint orbh worker show

```text
flint orbh worker show [options] <ref>
```

Show one worker in full

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | Worker id, backing session id, or label |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the worker as JSON |

##### flint orbh worker collect

```text
flint orbh worker collect [options] <ref>
```

Read a worker's updates from your own durable cursor, without waking it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | Worker id, backing session id, or label |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--peek` | Show new updates without advancing your cursor |
| `--all` | Show the full history without advancing your cursor |
| `--json` | Print the collected window as JSON |

##### flint orbh worker say

```text
flint orbh worker say [options] <text...>
```

Post a conversational reply onto a worker's bound surfaces

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `text` | What to say |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--worker <ref>` | Worker id, backing session id, or label (default: this session) |

##### flint orbh worker kill

```text
flint orbh worker kill [options] <ref>
```

Kill a worker's backing session and tear down its bound surfaces

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `ref` | Worker id, backing session id, or label |

#### flint orbh procedure

```text
flint orbh procedure [options] [command]
```

Attach a deterministic step register to this session: ordered steps, forced evidence check-ins, verb-side enforcement. Self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID.

Subcommands: `start`, `step`, `skip`, `show`, `list`, `finish`, `abandon`.

##### flint orbh procedure start

```text
flint orbh procedure start [options]
```

Attach a new procedure to a session (refused while one is already active)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--title <title>` | Human title for the procedure |
| `--from <file>` | Extract ordered steps from a markdown/text file (numbered items, else top-level bullets, else ## headings; fenced code blocks are ignored; a "(skippable)" annotation on a step line marks it skippable and is stripped from the title) |
| `--steps <list>` | Semicolon-separated step titles; a trailing "?" marks a step skippable |
| `--enforced` | Refuse `session return --finish` while required steps are outstanding |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID (a dispatcher may start a procedure for a session it launched) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh procedure step

```text
flint orbh procedure step [options] <n>
```

Complete the current step with evidence and advance the cursor (self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `n` | Step number — must be the current step |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--update <evidence>` | What you actually did — refused when empty |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh procedure skip

```text
flint orbh procedure skip [options] <n>
```

Skip the current step (skippable only) with a reason and advance the cursor (self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `n` | Step number — must be the current step |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--reason <why>` | Why this step is being skipped — refused when empty |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh procedure show

```text
flint orbh procedure show [options]
```

Show a session's active procedure (or its most recently updated one)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Dump the raw record |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID (read-only) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh procedure list

```text
flint orbh procedure list [options]
```

List a session's procedures (--all: every procedure in this Orb store)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--all` | List every procedure in the store, not just this session's |
| `--session <id>` | Target session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID (read-only) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh procedure finish

```text
flint orbh procedure finish [options]
```

Close the active procedure, refused while required steps are outstanding (self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh procedure abandon

```text
flint orbh procedure abandon [options]
```

Abandon the active procedure with a reason — the honest escape hatch (self-targets via ORBH_SESSION_ID)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--reason <why>` | Why the procedure is being abandoned — refused when empty |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint orbh workflow

```text
flint orbh workflow [options] [command]
```

Durable cross-session chaining: arm clauses on a session return or a procedure step, and let a chokepoint fire them.

Subcommands: `dynamic`, `after`, `after-procedure`, `arm`, `manage`, `show`, `list`, `close`, `cancel`, `fire-event`.

##### flint orbh workflow dynamic

```text
flint orbh workflow dynamic [options] [command]
```

Author a workflow on the spot, with no shipped definition behind it

Subcommands: `start`.

###### flint orbh workflow dynamic start

```text
flint orbh workflow dynamic start [options]
```

Start an empty dynamic workflow instance; hang clauses on it with `workflow after`

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--title <title>` | Human title for the workflow |
| `--session <id>` | Owning session id; defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `--max-children <n>` | Cap on children this workflow may launch (default 16) |
| `--json` | Emit the created instance as JSON |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow after

```text
flint orbh workflow after [options] <session-id>
```

Arm a clause on a session's NEXT return (an already-returned run does not count)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `session-id` | Subject session id (full or partial) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--then <prompt>` | Prompt to launch when the clause fires; may contain \{\{...\}\} tokens resolved at fire time, including \{\{result:subject\}\} for the triggering subject's own result |
| `--on <edge>` | Edge to take: finished \| failed \| any (default: "finished") |
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | Agent target for the launched child (default: 'default', the first available target of the default target list at launch) |
| `--wf <id>` | Append to this workflow instance instead of creating a fresh one |
| `--label <label>` | Child label, referenced by \{\{result:`<label>`\}\}; defaults to the clause id ('subject' is reserved) |
| `--title <title>` | Title for the launched child session |
| `--session <id>` | Owning session id for a freshly created instance; defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow after-procedure

```text
flint orbh workflow after-procedure [options] <procedure-id>
```

Arm a clause on a procedure step or on the procedure finishing (arming ahead of the procedure existing is legal)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `procedure-id` | Subject procedure id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--then <prompt>` | Prompt to launch when the clause fires; may contain \{\{...\}\} tokens resolved at fire time, including \{\{result:subject\}\} for the triggering subject's own result |
| `--step <n>` | Fire on this step settling; omit to fire when the procedure finishes |
| `--on <edge>` | Edge to take: finished \| failed \| any (default: "finished") |
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | Agent target for the launched child (default: 'default', the first available target of the default target list at launch) |
| `--wf <id>` | Append to this workflow instance instead of creating a fresh one |
| `--label <label>` | Child label, referenced by \{\{result:`<label>`\}\}; defaults to the clause id ('subject' is reserved) |
| `--title <title>` | Title for the launched child session |
| `--session <id>` | Owning session id for a freshly created instance; defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow arm

```text
flint orbh workflow arm [options] <name-or-path>
```

Arm a STATIC workflow from a shipped definition (by name) or a definition file (by path). Every trigger subject is resolved and anchored now; action prompts stay templates until they fire.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name-or-path` | Definition name under `<orbRoot>`/workflows/definitions, or a path to a definition JSON |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--args <k=v>` | Definition argument override; repeat to add more (default: []) |
| `--session <id>` | Owning session id; defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID |
| `--max-children <n>` | Override the definition's child cap (default 16) |
| `--json` | Emit the armed instance as JSON |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow manage

```text
flint orbh workflow manage [options] <id>
```

Attach a manager to a workflow. The manager is an event CONSUMER woken with a digest — it never collects children and never advances clauses.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Workflow instance id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | Agent target used when the engine has to spawn the manager |
| `--session <id>` | Attach this EXISTING session as the manager instead of spawning one |
| `--wake-on <csv>` | Signals to wake on (default clause-failed,child-failed,all-clauses-done); known: clause-fired,clause-done,clause-failed,child-failed,all-clauses-done |
| `--spawn <policy>` | eager \| on-first-wake (default: "on-first-wake") |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow show

```text
flint orbh workflow show [options] <id>
```

Render a workflow instance: derived status, clauses, children, context and journal

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Workflow instance id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Emit the raw instance plus derivedStatus and per-child outcome kinds |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow list

```text
flint orbh workflow list [options]
```

List workflow instances in this scope, most recently updated first

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Emit the list as JSON |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow close

```text
flint orbh workflow close [options] <id>
```

Mark a workflow closed — its duty is discharged; armed clauses stop matching

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Workflow instance id (full or unique prefix) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow cancel

```text
flint orbh workflow cancel [options] <id>
```

Mark a workflow cancelled — abandoned deliberately; armed clauses stop matching

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

##### flint orbh workflow fire-event

```text
flint orbh workflow fire-event [options]
```

Manually drive the firing chokepoint. The harness chokepoints (session return, orbh end, procedure step/skip/finish/abandon) already call fireWorkflowEvent in-process; this verb is that same entry point, driven by hand for testing and manual recovery.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--kind <kind>` | session-returned \| procedure-step \| procedure-finished |
| `--session <id>` | Subject session id (with --kind session-returned) |
| `--procedure <id>` | Subject procedure id (with the procedure kinds) |
| `--step <n>` | Step number (with --kind procedure-step) |
| `--outcome <outcome>` | procedure-step: done \| skipped \| failed; procedure-finished: finished \| abandoned |
| `--json` | Emit the fire report as JSON |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint orbh compact

```text
flint orbh compact [options] [command]
```

Agent-authored compaction, in three verbs. `compact start` prints the handoff contract and the path to write it to, holds the pager, and marks the session [Compacting...]; you then write the handoff with your own tools. `compact handoff` is TURN-ENDING: it validates what you wrote, ends this context, and relaunches the SAME session from it; `--into <runtime/profile> [--account <name>]` selects the runtime, profile, and optional account for that fresh run. Interactive panes keep their manager and swap only the harness child. `compact finish` is run by the RELAUNCHED context once it has read the handoff and every FILES path — it clears [Compacting...] and releases the hold. `compact abort` backs out before handoff.

Subcommands: `start`, `handoff`, `test`, `finish`, `abort`.

##### flint orbh compact start

```text
flint orbh compact start [options]
```

Hold the pager and prepare the compaction handoff. Request continuation on supported Codex managers. Keep this context alive.

##### flint orbh compact handoff

```text
flint orbh compact handoff [options]
```

TURN-ENDING: validate the handoff, end this context, and relaunch the same session from it. Optionally select a different runtime/profile/account for the fresh run.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--into <runtime/profile>` | Relaunch the fresh run under this profile registry key (cross-runtime allowed) |
| `--account <name>` | Use this account for the relaunched run (requires --into) |

##### flint orbh compact test

```text
flint orbh compact test [options]
```

Show the compaction curtain on this pane for a few seconds, then hand the pane back unchanged. Compacts nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--duration <ms>` | How long to hold the pane (900–30000ms, default 4500) |

##### flint orbh compact finish

```text
flint orbh compact finish [options]
```

Run by the RELAUNCHED context once it has read the handoff and every FILES path: clears [Compacting...] and releases the pager hold.

##### flint orbh compact abort

```text
flint orbh compact abort [options]
```

Back out of a compaction started but not handed off: releases the pager hold and clears [Compacting...].

|---|---|
| `--resume` | Release the previous claim for explicit manual recovery. Use only after the previous execution stopped. |

#### flint orbh switch

```text
flint orbh switch [options] <mode> [id]
```

Switch a session between interactive and headless mode; both landings come up live

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `mode` | Target mode (interactive\|headless) |
| `id` | Session ID (full or partial); defaults to ORBH_SESSION_ID inside a harness |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--force` | Adopt a subagent even though a live collector is blocked on it (interactive only) |
| `--here` | Attach this terminal to the relaunched session instead of just printing the attach command (interactive only) |
| `--no-launch` | Flip to interactive without starting anything; print the open command (interactive only) |
| `--await` | Land the released session awaiting, with the pickup note held for its next wake (headless only) |
| `--resume <prompt>` | Append this prompt after the pickup prompt on the resumed turn (headless only) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--orb-root <dir>` | Scope this command to an Orb store directory (overrides ORBH_ORB_ROOT_DIR and cwd) |
| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

| `--at <scope>` | Scope this command by name: 'flint:`<name>`' (registered Flint) or 'machine' (~/.orb). The human spelling of --orb-root. |
| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

| `--compact` | Distill the source using the compaction prompt before seeding the fork |
| `--attach` | Attach this terminal immediately after the detached fork starts |

| `--model <model>` | Model override |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
