| `--create-store` | Permit an explicitly scoped store that does not exist yet to be created |

### flint orbcode

```text
flint orbcode [options] [command]
```

OrbCode views: a view of a product with the proof of Orbtest. list, view, check, diff, history, and parts write nothing; review, apply, discard, restore, set, and remove write.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to the Flint (default: auto-detect) |

Subcommands: `list`, `view`, `check`, `review`, `diff`, `apply`, `discard`, `history`, `restore`, `set`, `remove`, `parts`.

#### flint orbcode list

```text
flint orbcode list [options]
```

List the views of each OrbCode project with their question, shape, lifetime, curation, proof counts, and finding counts. Writes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--project <name>` | Only the views of this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-view-list/1) |

#### flint orbcode view

```text
flint orbcode view [options] [view]
```

Show one view (or one candidate with --candidate) joined with the stories and the proof of Orbtest and with Git: each node with its proof state, its criteria, its cases, its related cases, and its findings. Writes nothing.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view (not with --candidate) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--candidate <id>` | Show a candidate as a view, with its proof |
| `--checkout <dir>` | Read the code and the Git history of this worktree of the codebase in place of its primary checkout; the Orbtest definitions and runs come from its product root when it has them, else from the primary checkout |
| `--project <name>` | Only the views of this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-view/1) |

#### flint orbcode check

```text
flint orbcode check [options] [view]
```

Print the findings of the views (format, code-ref-missing, story-missing, review-due, anchor-unknown, never-reviewed, no-contract, part-missing, no-part). Exits 1 when a finding has the level error. Writes nothing.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of one view (default: every view) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--paths <path...>` | Only the views with a node whose code-refs match one of these paths: an absolute path matches the code-refs of the codebase of its Git repository (also @\<Codebase name>/`<path>`); a relative path is a path of the codebase of the project |
| `--candidate <id>` | Check one candidate file as a view |
| `--checkout <dir>` | Read the code and the Git history of this worktree of the codebase in place of its primary checkout; the Orbtest definitions and runs come from its product root when it has them, else from the primary checkout |
| `--project <name>` | Only the views of this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-check/1) |

#### flint orbcode review

```text
flint orbcode review [options] <view>
```

Write the review anchor of the nodes of a view: the commit and the hashes of the claim and of the references of each node. With no --node, each node with a node block.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--node <id...>` | Only these nodes |
| `--commit <sha>` | The commit of the anchor (default: the HEAD of the codebase) |
| `--project <name>` | Only the views of this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-review/1) |

#### flint orbcode diff

```text
flint orbcode diff [options] [view]
```

Compare a candidate with its view by node id: the added, removed, moved, and changed nodes, and whether an apply is a conflict now. Writes nothing.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view (the view_id of the candidate for a new view); not needed with --against-candidate |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--candidate <id>` | The candidate id |
| `--against-candidate <id>` | Compare with another candidate of the same view in place of the view |
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-diff/1) |

#### flint orbcode apply

```text
flint orbcode apply [options] <view>
```

Replace a view with a candidate only when the view did not change after the candidate was made (the base_hash of the candidate). Then remove the candidate. A conflict writes nothing and exits 1. An apply by an agent sets the curation proposed; an apply by a person keeps the curation of the view.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view (the view_id of the candidate for a new view) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--candidate <id>` | The candidate id |
| `--by <agent\|person>` | Who applies: agent sets the curation proposed; person keeps the curation of the view (default: "agent") |
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-apply/1) |

#### flint orbcode discard

```text
flint orbcode discard [options]
```

Remove a candidate file.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--candidate <id>` | The candidate id |
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-discard/1) |

#### flint orbcode history

```text
flint orbcode history [options] <view>
```

List the replaced forms of a view in History/, the newest first. An apply keeps the content that it replaces there. Writes nothing.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-history/1) |

#### flint orbcode restore

```text
flint orbcode restore [options] <view>
```

Make a candidate from a replaced form of a view (History/). Then apply it as any candidate: the view does not change here.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--from <history-id>` | The history id (flint orbcode history) |
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-restore/1) |

#### flint orbcode set

```text
flint orbcode set [options] <view>
```

Set the lifetime (draft or kept) and the curation (proposed or accepted) of a view: the decision of a person. Only these keys of the file change.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--lifetime <lifetime>` | draft or kept |
| `--curation <curation>` | proposed or accepted |
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-fields/1) |

#### flint orbcode remove

```text
flint orbcode remove [options] <view>
```

Remove a draft view and its candidates. A kept view is refused: set --lifetime draft first. The view goes to History/ first (flint orbcode restore brings it back).

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `view` | The UUID or the name of the view |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--project <name>` | Only this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-remove/1) |

#### flint orbcode parts

```text
flint orbcode parts [options]
```

List the static parts of each OrbCode project (the artifacts of Map/) with the processes that pass through each part (views of the shape flow or streams, nodes that name the part) and the count of the code-refs that name no file. Writes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--project <name>` | Only the parts of this project |
| `--json` | Output one JSON line (schema orbcode-parts/1) |

### flint ite

```text
flint ite [options] [command]
```

Read and change programs, maps, views, contacts, observations, and jobs.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | The Flint folder. The default is the current Flint. |

Subcommands: `flow`, `system`, `statements`, `evidence`, `instruments`, `read`, `notice`, `log`, `brief`, `vitals`, `prompt`, `adopt`, `revision`, `list`, `frameworks`, `map`, `view`, `check`, `create`, `rename`, `archive`, `part`, `include`, `link`, `contact`, `run`, `act`, `observe`, `observations`, `focus`, `diff`, `apply`, `discard`, `job`.

#### flint ite flow

```text
flint ite flow [options] [command]
```

Read flows and run them by hand.

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `start`, `runs`, `status`, `done`, `answer`, `skip`, `dispatch`, `reconcile`, `waive`, `approve`, `pause`, `resume`, `cancel`.

##### flint ite flow list

```text
flint ite flow list [options] <program>
```

List the flows of a program. Write nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |

##### flint ite flow show

```text
flint ite flow show [options] <view>
```

Read the resolved flow and its refusals. Write nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |

##### flint ite flow start

```text
flint ite flow start [options] <program> <view>
```

Start a flow run and save its snapshot.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--title <text>` | The title of the run. |
| `--input <name=value>` | Give a run input. Repeat for each input. Separate notes with semicolons. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the run started. |

##### flint ite flow runs

```text
flint ite flow runs [options] <program>
```

List the runs of a program. Write nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--status <status>` | One of: active, paused, succeeded, failed, cancelled. |

##### flint ite flow status

```text
flint ite flow status [options] <run>
```

Read the checklist, active step, inputs, and output form.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `run` | The run id, its prefix of at least eight characters, or its title. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |

##### flint ite flow done

```text
flint ite flow done [options] <run>
```

Complete the active visit with its outputs.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--output <name=value>` | Give a text, number, or choice output. |
| `--note <name=title>` | Give the title of one output note. |
| `--text <name=text>` | Give the body of an output note. |
| `--notes <name=lines>` | Give note titles separated by semicolons. |
| `--input <name=value>` | Give an input that the run does not supply. |

##### flint ite flow answer

```text
flint ite flow answer [options] <run> <outcome>
```

Answer the active decision.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |

##### flint ite flow skip

```text
flint ite flow skip [options] <run>
```

Skip a step that has no required output.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--reason <text>` | Why this step can be skipped. |

##### flint ite flow dispatch

```text
flint ite flow dispatch [options] <run>
```

Dispatch the active visit: an agent or a command step, or the Begin of a person step whose effect needs evidence.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |

##### flint ite flow reconcile

```text
flint ite flow reconcile [options] <run>
```

Check the pending effects of a run against the system log now, or close an unknown attempt by a decision of a person.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--attempt <id>` | The attempt. The default is each open attempt of the run. |
| `--decision <decision>` | For an unknown attempt: retry, failed, or wait-evidence. |
| `--reason <text>` | The reason of the decision. |

##### flint ite flow waive

```text
flint ite flow waive [options] <run>
```

Waive one pending or unknown effect with a reason (a person only). A waiver is not a confirmation.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--attempt <id>` | The attempt whose effect you waive. |
| `--reason <text>` | Why the effect does not need evidence. |

##### flint ite flow approve

```text
flint ite flow approve [options] <run>
```

Grant or refuse the approval of the step that waits (a person only). The visit, the revision, and the digest come from a read right before the write.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--step <id>` | The step that waits for the approval. |
| `--refuse` | Refuse the approval. |
| `--reason <text>` | The reason of the decision. |
| `--revision <revision>` | The revision that you reviewed. The default is the revision of the read before the write. |
| `--digest <digest>` | The inputs digest that you reviewed. The default is the digest of the read before the write. |

##### flint ite flow pause

```text
flint ite flow pause [options] <run>
```

Pause a run.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--reason <text>` | The reason for this action. |

##### flint ite flow resume

```text
flint ite flow resume [options] <run>
```

Resume a run.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--reason <text>` | The reason for this action. |

##### flint ite flow cancel

```text
flint ite flow cancel [options] <run>
```

Cancel a run.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--request-id <id>` | Use this request id for a retry. The default is a new UUID. |
| `--expected-seq <number>` | Require this last sequence. The default is the sequence read before this command. |
| `--at <time>` | The ISO time when the action occurred. |
| `--reason <text>` | The reason for this action. |

#### flint ite read

```text
flint ite read [options] <program>
```

Read instruments now. The system log gets the observations and one read record of each instrument.

|---|---|
| `program` | The name or id of the program. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |

#### flint ite notice

```text
flint ite notice [options] <program>
```

Take one notice of an event source. A notice is not evidence: a read of the instrument follows.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `program` | The name or id of the program. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--instrument <id>` | The instrument that the notice is for. |

```text

```

Show the records of the system log. Write nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--since-seq <n>` | Only the records after this sequence, the oldest first. |
| `--kind <kind>` | Only one kind: observation, read, notice, ack, brief-opened, escalation, prompt, detection, clearance. |
| `--limit <n>` | The most records (default 200, at most 5000). |

#### flint ite brief

```text

```

Show the brief: what changed, what is at risk, what is old, what nobody watches, and what waits. Write nothing.

|---|---|
| `program` | The name or id of the program. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--since <duration>` | The start of the window, for example 24h, or an ISO time. |
| `--markdown` | Print the brief as Markdown. |

Subcommands: `ack`.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

|---|---|

##### flint ite revision propose

```text
flint ite revision propose [options] <program>
```

Propose a revision of one file of the system. It writes the revision file only.

|---|---|

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |

| `--content-file <path>` | A file with the full new bytes of the target. |
| `--reason <text>` | Why the change is needed. |
| `--subject <id>` | The part, statement, or flow that the revision is about. |
| `--trigger <trigger>` | One of: person, repeated-failure, goal-at-risk, surprise. The default is person. |
| `--base-hash <hash>` | The hash of the target that you read. The default is the hash of the target now (none for a new file). |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

| `--reason <text>` | Why you revert (revert only). |
| `--protected` | Apply a revision that changes a protected field (a person only). |

|---|---|
| `program` | The name or id of the program. |
| `name` | The new name of the program. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--base-hash <hash>` | Refuse the rename if the program file has a different hash. |

#### flint ite archive

Move a program to Mesh/Archive/Programs. Delete no file.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `program` | The name or id of the program. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--base-hash <hash>` | Refuse the archive if the program file has a different hash. |

#### flint ite part

```text
flint ite part [options] [command]
```

Add or change a part of a program.

Subcommands: `add`, `set`.

##### flint ite part add

```text

```

| Option | Description |

| `--title <text>` | The title of the part. |
| `--parent <id>` | The id of the parent node. |
| `--text <text>` | The text of the part. |
| `--field <key=value>` | Set a field. Repeat this flag for more fields. A value can be JSON. |

##### flint ite part set

```text

```

Change the given fields of a part. Other fields stay.

| `--parent <id>` | The parent node. An empty value removes the parent. |
| `--field <key=value>` | Set a field. Repeat for more fields. JSON null removes the field. |
| `--base-hash <hash>` | Refuse the change if the note has a different hash. |

#### flint ite include

Put an existing Mesh note on the map. With --remove, take it off and keep the note.

| `--remove` | Remove the note from include. Keep the note file. |

Add a link. With --remove, remove the link.

| Option | Description |

#### flint ite contact

```text
flint ite contact [options] [command]
```

Set where a node touches reality.

Subcommands: `add`, `remove`.

##### flint ite contact add

```text

```

| Argument | Description |

| `node` | The node id. |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON value. |
| `--document <id>` | The map, or the id of a view. (default: "map") |
| `--kind <kind>` | reference, file, command, http, mesh, orbtest, agent, human, or note. |
| `--id <id>` | A mapping contact id. The default uses claim words, prompt words, or command words, then the kind. Duplicates get a number. Wikilinks use a computed id. |
| `--claim <text>` | The statement that the contact checks. |
| `--run <command>` | The shell command of a command contact. |
| `--cwd <dir>` | The working folder of the command. |
| `--url <url>` | The URL of an HTTP contact. |
| `--path <path>` | The file path of a file contact. |
| `--ref <note>` | The Mesh note name. Reference and note contacts store a wikilink and no other fields. |
| `--prompt <text>` | The question for an agent or person. |
| `--fresh-for <duration>` | How long an observation stays fresh, for example 7d. |
| `--direction <direction>` | observe or act. The default is observe. |
| `--query <json>` | The Mesh query as a JSON object. |
| `--expect <json>` | The expected result as a JSON object. |
| `--story <id>` | An Orbtest story id. Repeat for more stories. |
| `--criterion <address>` | An Orbtest criterion address. Repeat for more criteria. |

##### flint ite contact remove

### flint tinderbox

```text
flint tinderbox [options] [command]
```

Manage a box of Flints

```text
A box has three files: tinderbox.toml (the intent), tinderbox.json (the record),
and .tinderbox/ (the facts of this machine).
```

Subcommands: `init`, `start`, `import`, `add`, `remove`, `rename`, `dissolve`, `sync`, `repo`, `git`, `org`, `status`, `check`, `heal`.

#### flint tinderbox init

```text
flint tinderbox init [options] [name]
```

Make a box folder here: a new empty box with `<name>`, or a clone of the box at --from `<url>` followed by the local sync

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The box name (with --from, the name in tinderbox.toml is used) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--from <url>` | Clone the box from this Git URL, then run the local sync |

#### flint tinderbox start

```text
flint tinderbox start [options] <name> [path]
```

Make a new box and move the current Flint into it as its first owned member

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The box name |
| `path` | The folder that gets the new box (default: the parent folder of the current Flint) |

#### flint tinderbox import

```text
flint tinderbox import [options] <name> [source]
```

Move a Flint of the roster into this box as an owned member, or declare it from `<source>` when the roster does not have it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The name of the Flint in the roster |
| `source` | A Git URL or "registry:`<name>`": declares the member with no move when the roster does not have `<name>` |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-open` | Do not open the Flint in Obsidian after the move |
| `--yes` | Move the Flint with no confirmation prompt |

#### flint tinderbox add

```text
flint tinderbox add [options] <name> <source>
```

Declare a member without cloning or moving it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The member name in tinderbox.toml |
| `source` | A Git source (an owned member) or "registry:`<name>`" (a reference member) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

```text
add writes the declaration to tinderbox.toml, refreshes .gitignore, and
records the member in tinderbox.json when its id is known. It makes
.tinderbox/ and the roster row of the box when they are absent. The next
flint tinderbox sync clones an owned member or references a roster member.
```

#### flint tinderbox remove

```text
flint tinderbox remove [options] <name>
```

Remove a member and keep its folder, unless --move-out moves it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The member name in tinderbox.toml |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--move-out <dir>` | Move the member folder out of the box into `<dir>` |
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

```text
The member leaves tinderbox.toml and tinderbox.json. The references that the
box wired are stripped. The folder and its roster row stay, except with
--move-out: the folder then moves into <dir>, and the roster row follows it.
```

#### flint tinderbox rename

```text
flint tinderbox rename [options] <from> [to]
```

Rename a member (its declaration, its record entry, its flint.toml name, and its folder), or rename the box with --tinderbox

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `from` | The member name, or the new box name with --tinderbox |
| `to` | The new member name (not with --tinderbox) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--tinderbox` | Rename the box, its folder, and its roster row |
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

#### flint tinderbox dissolve

```text
flint tinderbox dissolve [options]
```

Remove the box and keep the member folders

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--move-to <dir>` | Move every member folder out of the box into `<dir>` |
| `--force` | Dissolve also when a member has uncommitted or unpushed work |
| `--dry-run` | Show the plan and change nothing |
| `--yes` | Dissolve with no confirmation prompt |
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

```text
Dissolve backs up and removes tinderbox.toml and tinderbox.json, removes the
roster row, and strips the wiring. The member folders, .git, and .tinderbox/
(with the backups) stay.
```

#### flint tinderbox sync

```text
flint tinderbox sync [options]
```

Make the box match its intent on this machine, then run the local sync (`flint sync`) in each selected member

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Show the plan of the box and of each selected member and change nothing |
| `--no-git` | Not needed: tinderbox sync does no Git transport (retired) |
| `--no-update` | Not needed: tinderbox sync does not move a lock (retired) |
| `--json` | Print the operation report of the box and of each member as one JSON value |
| `--only <names...>` | Sync only these members (the repos are not synced) |
| `--skip <names...>` | Do not sync these members (the repos are not synced) |

```text
Clones a missing owned member, references a roster member, wires the
connections and the repos, and records the box in tinderbox.json. It makes
no Obsidian vault registry entry: a vault enters the registry when Obsidian
opens it. Then runs `flint sync` in each selected member. It never asks
the registry, never moves a lock, and never touches origin. Undeclared folders
stay where they are.
```

#### flint tinderbox repo

```text
flint tinderbox repo [options] [command]
```

Manage the repos of the box: codebases that are not Flints and that the box shares with its members

Subcommands: `add`, `remove`, `list`.

##### flint tinderbox repo add

```text
flint tinderbox repo add [options] <name> <source>
```

Declare a repo in tinderbox.toml and clone it into Repos/, or reference it at path:`<dir>`

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The repo name (the codebase reference name in each member) |
| `source` | A Git URL (cloned into Repos/) or "path:`<dir>`" (a reference repo) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--exposed-to <value>` | The members that get a codebase reference: "all" or a comma-separated list of member names (default: "all") (default: "all") |
| `--mode <value>` | "own" (clone into Repos/) or "reference" (use the path as it is) |
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

##### flint tinderbox repo remove

```text
flint tinderbox repo remove [options] <name>
```

Remove a repo from tinderbox.toml and strip its codebase reference from each member

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The repo name in tinderbox.toml |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--purge` | Also delete the clone under Repos/ (an owned repo only) |
| `--yes` | Delete the clone with no confirmation prompt |
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

##### flint tinderbox repo list

```text
flint tinderbox repo list [options]
```

List the repos: name, mode, source, path, the members that get it, and whether it is on this machine

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--wide` | Show every column at full width |

#### flint tinderbox git

```text
flint tinderbox git [options] [command]
```

The transport of the box: exchange the history of the box repo and of each member with origin

Subcommands: `sync`, `status`, `resume`, `resolve`, `publish`.

##### flint tinderbox git sync

```text
flint tinderbox git sync [options]
```

Exchange the box and member histories with origin

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--force-remote` | In each member, take the remote as truth: keep local work that merges, discard what does not |
| `--force-local` | In each member, take the local side as truth: overlapping changes resolve in favour of local commits |
| `--no-sync` | Transport only: do not run the local sync of the box or of the members |
| `--only <names...>` | Sync only these members (the box repo still syncs) |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as one JSON value |

```text
The box goes first: checkpoint, fetch, integrate. Then the local sync of the
box. Then the members, 4 at a time, each with the flow of `flint git sync`.
Then the second checkpoint of the box, and the push. A reference member is
skipped: its own Flint moves its history. A conflict holds the rebase; run
`flint tinderbox git resolve <member>`. A merge, a cherry-pick, or a revert
that holds a member or the box blocks it (exit 2): finish it with Git first.
```

##### flint tinderbox git status

```text
flint tinderbox git status [options]
```

Show the Git journal of the last or interrupted `git sync` and the Git state of each member, with no change

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the operation report as one JSON value |

##### flint tinderbox git resume

```text
flint tinderbox git resume [options]
```

Continue the last `flint tinderbox git sync` for each member that it did not finish

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-sync` | Transport only: do not run the local sync of the box or of the members |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as one JSON value |

##### flint tinderbox git resolve

```text
flint tinderbox git resolve [options] <member>
```

Fix one member that `git sync` left blocked: continue its held rebase, or sync it again with --local or --remote

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `member` | The member name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--local` | Take the local side: overlapping changes resolve in favour of local commits |
| `--remote` | Take the remote side: keep local work that merges, discard what does not |
| `--replay` | After a remote history rewrite, replay local-only commits |
| `--force-local` | Renamed: use --local (retired) |
| `--force-remote` | Renamed: use --remote (retired) |
| `--no-sync` | Transport only: do not run the local sync of the member |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as one JSON value |

##### flint tinderbox git publish

```text
flint tinderbox git publish [options] <url>
```

Publish the box repo to a remote: set origin, name the branch main, commit pending changes, and push

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `url` | Git remote URL (e.g. git@github.com:owner/repo.git) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--yes` | Change origin, rename the branch, and commit with no confirmation prompt (needed with --json or with no terminal) |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as one JSON value |

#### flint tinderbox org

```text
flint tinderbox org [options] [command]
```

The org of the box and of its owned members

Subcommands: `set`.

##### flint tinderbox org set

```text
flint tinderbox org set [options] <org>
```

Set the org of the box and of each owned member: print the plan, and write it with --apply. `none` removes the org

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `org` | The org slug, the org id, or none |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--id <uuid>` | The id of the org. The admin of the org gives it. Not needed when this machine knows the org |
| `--apply` | Write the plan. Without it nothing is changed |
| `--json` | Print the report as one JSON value |

#### flint tinderbox status

```text
flint tinderbox status [options]
```

Show the box (its name, its org, its last sync) and the state of each member and each connection

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the status as one JSON value |
| `--wide` | Show every column at full width |

#### flint tinderbox check

```text
flint tinderbox check [options]
```

Compare the intent of the box with this machine and give one next command for each finding, with no change

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the findings, the status, and the next commands as one JSON value |

#### flint tinderbox heal

```text
flint tinderbox heal [options]
```

Repair the drift that `flint tinderbox check` finds (with a backup of the box files), then run the local sync of the box

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Show every effect of the run (each repair and the local sync), and each finding that heal does not repair, and change nothing |
| `--yes` | Repair with no confirmation prompt |
| `--json` | Print the result as one JSON value, in the shape of `sync --json` |

### flint orbit

```text
flint orbit [options] [command]
```

#### flint orbit serve

```text

```

Start the Orbit server. One Orbit runs for each NUU home: a second start shows the URL of the live Orbit and exits

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--port <port>` | The port of Orbit (default: ORBIT_PORT, else 13070) |
| `--no-open` | Do not open the browser (ORBIT_OPEN=0 does the same) |

### flint blacksmith

```text
flint blacksmith [options] [command]
```

Manage the Blacksmith daemon of this machine: it starts the Flint servers and the Orbh orchestrator

Subcommands: `daemon`, `status`, `ls`, `kill`, `reload-plugins`, `restart-server`, `restart-steel`, `stop`, `restart`, `logs`.

#### flint blacksmith daemon


Start the Blacksmith daemon in the background. --foreground: run it in this terminal

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--foreground` | Run the daemon process in this terminal (do not detach). The long-running process entry |
| `--port <port>` | The port of the daemon (with --foreground). 0 takes a free port; the endpoint projection of the home records it. Default 13030 |

#### flint blacksmith status

```text
flint blacksmith status [options]
```

Show the status of the Blacksmith daemon


List resources registered with Blacksmith

| `--flint-path <path>` | Filter by Flint path |

#### flint blacksmith kill


Terminate and deregister a resource by token or Flint path

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|


Reload the NUU Flint plugin in every open Obsidian vault

|---|---|

#### flint blacksmith restart-server

Restart flint-server daemons: graceful shutdown, then respawn via Blacksmith

|---|---|
| `--all` | Restart every flint-server |

#### flint blacksmith restart-steel

```text

```

Restart the one Steel of this computer with the active steel binary: stop it, and Blacksmith starts it again on the same port

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

#### flint blacksmith stop

```text

```

Stop the Blacksmith daemon

### flint setup

```text
flint setup [options] [command]
```

Set up this machine in seven steps: your Name and the machine, the Git author, the tools, the agent default, your Computer Flint, the Guide, and the open of your Computer Flint with your first agent session. Setup needs internet access: with no network it stops before the first step

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <name>` | Your Name (the author of your notes) |
| `--machine-name <name>` | The branch prefix of this machine (default: from the hostname) |
| `--machine-proper-name <name>` | The machine display name (the name people read, such as "Ada Laptop"). It renames the Computer Flint too |
| `--git-email <email>` | The email of the Git author, with no question. Setup writes it only when Git has no email |
| `--no-open` | Do not open the Computer Flint in Obsidian (the onboarding session then runs in this terminal) |
| `--guide` | Clone the Guide of NUU Cognition with no question |
| `--no-guide` | Do not clone the Guide (later: flint setup guide) |
| `--font` | Install the terminal font JetBrainsMono Nerd Font with no question |
| `--no-font` | Do not install the terminal font (later: flint setup font) |
| `-y, --yes` | Ask no question. Every open input takes its default, setup installs the terminal font, and setup clones the Guide |
| `--json` | Output one JSON result envelope on stdout. Ask no question. The text for a person goes to stderr |

Subcommands: `shell-integration`, `url-handler`, `computer`, `guide`, `font`, `onboard`, `handoff`.


| Option | Description |
|---|---|

### flint doctor

```text
flint doctor [options]
```

Check this machine and the current Flint, and give one next command for each problem. Starts no agent

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Check the Flint at this path (default: the Flint of the working folder) |
| `--json` | Print the checks as one JSON value |

```text
The checks: your identity and the NUU home, Git, Node.js, Obsidian, the
terminal of Obsidian (its PATH finds node and flint, its font, and python3),
the agent (the default target list of Orbh), the launcher record, the
Blacksmith daemon (a read: it starts no daemon), the Computer Flint, the Flint
registry, the Flint version (it names a newer release and never fails for it),
and the Orbh profiles.
Inside a Flint: its lock, the layout of flint.toml, its sync plan, its modules,
its Git state, and its Obsidian payload.
A check that needs judgement names flint fix; then the doctor writes one failure
record for it. It writes nothing else. Exit 0 when each check passes, else 1.
```

### flint fix

```text
flint fix [options] [record]
```

Start an agent that repairs the last failure of a Flint command (a repair session)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `record` | The failure record to repair: a number of flint fix --list (1 is the newest), a record id, or a record file |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--list` | List the failure records of this machine |
| `--json` | With --list: print one JSON value |

```text
When flint sync, flint create, flint git sync, flint shard install, flint setup,
or flint tinderbox sync fails, Flint writes a failure record and prints
"Or: flint fix". flint fix starts an interactive Orbh session on the default
target in your Computer Flint. The session reads the record and follows the
repair skill of the Flint shard. flint doctor checks this machine and starts no agent.
To report the failure to NUU Cognition: flint feedback --bug --with-failure.
```

### flint feedback

```text
flint feedback [options] [text...]
```

Send feedback about Flint to NUU Cognition: a bug, an idea, or a question

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `text` | The feedback. With no text, a terminal asks for it |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--bug` | The feedback is a bug report |
| `--idea` | The feedback is an idea |
| `--question` | The feedback is a question |
| `--title <text>` | The title of the report (default: the first line of the text) |
| `--with-failure` | Add the facts of the newest failure record of flint fix: the command words, the exit code, the error code, the version, and the time |
| `--email` | Send it as an email to hello@nuucognition.com in place of a GitHub issue |
| `--no-open` | Print the report and the link, and open nothing |
| `-y, --yes` | Open the link with no question |
| `--json` | Print the report and the link as one JSON value; opens nothing without --yes |

```text
flint feedback builds a report: your text, the kind, the Flint version, the OS
and the arch, and the Node.js version. It shows the report and asks before it
opens anything. Then your browser opens a new issue of
NUU-Cognition/flint-public with the report filled in, and you submit it.
--email opens an email to hello@nuucognition.com in your mail app.
Flint sends nothing itself.
Flint removes some secrets, but not all. Read the report.
Remove each secret before you send it.
Each line of your text that has a secret key, a secret flag, or an
Authorization or Cookie header loses the rest of the line. Private key blocks,
the user information of URLs, known tokens, and home folder paths go too.
--with-failure adds only the facts of the newest failure record (flint fix
--list): the command words, the exit code, the error code, the Flint version,
and the time. It adds no arguments, no output, no reason, and no paths.
A local copy of each report goes to the NUU home (flint/feedback/).
With no terminal, the command prints the report and the link only.

Examples:
  flint feedback --bug "flint sync stops on a fresh clone" --with-failure
  flint feedback --idea "A flint sync --watch mode"
  flint feedback --question "How do I share a shard with my org?" --no-open
To repair a failure on this machine, run flint fix.
```

### flint git

```text
flint git [options] [command] [args...]
```

Git operations for the Flint: init, sync, merge, resolve, publish, or passthrough to git

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `args` | Raw git arguments (passthrough); bare `flint git` shows the Flint git status |

```text
Anything that is not a listed subcommand passes through to git, run at the Flint root.

Examples:
  flint git status
  flint git sync -m "polish docs"
  flint git publish git@github.com:owner/repo.git
```

Subcommands: `init`, `sync`, `merge`, `resolve`, `publish`.

#### flint git init

```text
flint git init [options]
```

Initialize a git repository with the Flint Git policy: .gitignore, .gitattributes, and rerere

#### flint git sync

```text
flint git sync [options]
```

Exchange history with origin: checkpoint, fetch, integrate, run the local sync, push

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-m, --message <msg>` | Custom commit message |
| `--replay` | After a remote history rewrite, replay local-only commits onto the new history |
| `--force-remote` | Take the remote as truth: keep local work that merges, discard what does not |
| `--force-local` | Take the local side as truth: overlapping changes resolve in favour of local commits |
| `--no-sync` | Transport only: do not run the local sync |
| `--continue` | Continue a sync halted on a conflict (a bare re-run also continues) |
| `--abort` | Abort a halted sync: stop the in-progress rebase and restore the branch |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as JSON (with --abort: one JSON value) |

```text
Checkpoints all changes, fetches and diagnoses divergence, rebases or
fast-forwards, runs the local sync (`flint sync`), checkpoints the writes of
the local sync, and pushes. The push runs also when the local sync fails;
the writes of a failed local sync stay uncommitted, and the push sends the
integrated history as it is: when origin moved during that sync, the push is
rejected, and the next `flint git sync` sends it. `--no-sync` is transport
only. If the remote history was rewritten (force-push), sync realigns
automatically when no local-only commits exist, or halts and asks for
--replay to carry local commits over.

--force-remote makes the fetched remote branch the source of truth. Local
commits that replay cleanly are kept; overlapping changes are resolved in
favour of the remote, and anything that still won't merge is discarded.
A backup ref preserves committed parent history before integration.
Commit or stash changes in nested repositories before you use this flag.

--force-local is the mirror image: overlapping changes resolve in favour of
your local commits. Remote history is never rewritten — remote commits stay
in the base; only conflicting content takes the local side.

A conflict halts before the local sync. Edit the files (or use
`flint git resolve`), then re-run `flint git sync` (or `--continue`): it
finishes the integrate, runs the local sync, and pushes. `--abort` restores
the pre-sync branch and runs no sync. `--continue` does not take --replay,
--force-remote, or --force-local.

A git merge, cherry-pick, or revert that is in progress stops the sync
with exit 2, and nothing changes. Finish it or abort it with git first.
```

#### flint git merge

```text
flint git merge [options]
```

Integrate with origin and run the local sync, with no push: checkpoint, fetch, integrate, run the local sync

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-m, --message <msg>` | Custom commit message |
| `--replay` | After a remote history rewrite, replay local-only commits onto the new history |
| `--force-remote` | Overlapping changes resolve in favour of the remote |
| `--force-local` | Overlapping changes resolve in favour of local commits |
| `--no-sync` | Transport only: do not run the local sync |
| `--json` | Print the operation report as JSON |

```text
`flint git sync` with no push: it checkpoints, fetches, integrates, runs the
local sync, and checkpoints the writes of the local sync. Use it to converge
with the remote, inspect the result, then publish with `flint git sync`. A
conflict halts exactly like sync; resolve and re-run, or `flint git sync --abort`.
```

#### flint git resolve

```text
flint git resolve [options] [paths...]
```

During a conflict halt, resolve files by taking one side whole

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `paths` | Files to resolve (default: every conflicted file) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--local` | Take your local version of the paths |
| `--remote` | Take the remote version of the paths |

```text
Only valid while a sync is halted on a conflict (mid-rebase). Takes the
named side of each file whole — no marker editing, and it works for binary
files. A path is relative to the working directory. When every conflict is
resolved, run `flint git sync` to continue.
```

#### flint git publish

```text
flint git publish [options] <url>
```

Publish this Flint repository to a remote repository

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `url` | Remote repository URL |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--yes` | Confirm a change to the origin URL |

### flint foundation

```text

```

The process registry of NUU services and their memory usage.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

```text
Without a command, flint foundation runs list.
```

Subcommands: `list`, `inspect`, `logs`, `ghosts`, `totals`, `prune`, `version`, `storage`, `top`.

#### flint foundation list

```text
flint foundation list [--service <service>] [--owner <kind>:<id>] [--all] [--tree]
```

List the registered NUU processes, their state, and their RSS. The default command.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--service <service>` | Only the processes of this service. |
| `--owner <kind>:<id>` | Only the processes of this owner. The ID must match exactly. |
| `--all` | Also show the stale records. |
| `--tree` | Also show the unregistered descendants of the registered processes. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation inspect

```text
flint foundation inspect <PID|registration-UUID> [--tree]
```

Show one process: identity, record, owner, launch parent, observed parent, memory, and log path. A UUID selects its record, also a stale one. A PID selects the current process.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--tree` | Also show the descendants and the RSS sum of the tree. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation logs

```text
flint foundation logs [<registration-UUID>] [--service <service>] [--role <role>] [--event <prefix>]... [--level <level>] [--since <time>] [--limit <n>] [--list]
```

Read the event log of a NUU process: its events, filtered by service, event, level, and time. A log is \<NUU home>/foundation/logs/\<registration UUID>/events.jsonl. events.1.jsonl holds the lines before the last rotation. A process writes a log when it registers with events: true. A log stays after its process exits. Without a UUID, logs reads the newest log that matches --service and --role, of a live or an exited process. The prefix sync selects the events sync and sync.command.applied. It does not select syncx. prune --apply removes the logs of exited processes that are old or above the byte budget.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--list` | List the logs, newest first, with their state and size. |
| `--service <service>` | Read the newest log of this service. With --list: only the logs of this service. |
| `--role <role>` | Read the newest log of this role. With --list: only the logs of this role. |
| `--event <prefix>` | Only the events whose name starts with these whole words. Repeat it for more prefixes. |
| `--level <level>` | Only the events of this level or higher: debug, info, warn, or error. |
| `--since <time>` | Only the events at or after this time: a duration (30s, 10m, 2h, 1d) or an ISO time. |
| `--limit <n>` | Show the last `<n>` matching events, from 0 to 1000000. 0 shows all. Default: 200. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation ghosts

```text
flint foundation ghosts [--root <absolute-repo-or-install-root>]...
```

Find running processes of this user with NUU evidence and no verified record. The scan is heuristic. An unregistered detached child without current ancestry or recognizable arguments can remain undiscovered. Candidates need review. The command stops no process.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <path>` | Also accept NUU entrypoints below this absolute root. Repeat it for more roots. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation totals

```text
flint foundation totals [--service <service>] [--owner <kind>:<id>] [--tree]
```

Sum the RSS of the verified current processes, once per process, by service. RSS can count shared pages more than once.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--service <service>` | Only this service. |
| `--owner <kind>:<id>` | Only the processes of this owner. |
| `--tree` | Also count the unregistered descendants, in the service of their registered ancestor. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation prune

```text
flint foundation prune [--apply]
```

Preview the stale records, the old temporary files, and the old event logs that can be removed. The event log of an exited process goes when its newest file is older than 7 days. When all logs use more than 200 MiB, the oldest logs of exited processes go until the sum is 200 MiB or less. The log of a live or unknown process always stays. Prune removes only events.jsonl and events.1.jsonl, and then the empty folder. Prune never signals a process and never changes a service registry.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--apply` | Remove them after a fresh check. Unknown and invalid records stay. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation version

```text
flint foundation version
```

Show the CLI version, the record protocol, and the output schema.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation storage

```text
flint foundation storage [--no-flints] [--top <n>] [--timeout <ms>]
```

Measure the disk usage of the NUU home, of the Flints of this machine, and of their Orbh spools. The size is the disk usage: the allocated blocks. The measure does not follow a symbolic link, stays on the device of each area, and counts a file with more than one hard link one time. The command deletes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--no-flints` | Measure only the NUU home. Do not read the Flint registry. |
| `--top <n>` | Show the `<n>` largest spools of each Flint. Default: 5. |
| `--timeout <ms>` | Stop the measure after `<ms>` milliseconds. Default: 20000. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

#### flint foundation top

```text
flint foundation top [--interval <ms>] [--service <service>] [--owner <kind>:<id>] [--tree] [--sort cpu|rss|pid|service] [--no-storage] [--no-mouse] [--once]
```

Show a live screen of the NUU processes with their CPU, their memory, and the storage sizes. On a terminal, top shows a live view until you press q or Ctrl-C. Press ? for the keys. Tab moves the focus between the process table and the storage panel. The arrow keys, j, k, g, G, PgUp, and PgDn move the panel with the focus. The storage panel lists all areas; it starts folded. + (or =) opens the selected row and - folds it; e opens all rows, c folds all rows. Enter, the left and right arrow keys, and a click on [+] or [-] also open and fold a row. z gives the panel with the focus all rows. The mouse wheel scrolls the panel under the pointer. A click gives a panel the focus and selects a row. The key m turns the mouse reports off, so that the terminal can select text. You can also hold Option (macOS) or Shift to select text. With --json, --once, or no terminal, top takes two samples one interval apart, measures the storage, prints one reply, and ends. An agent uses top --json. CPU: 100 % is one full core. The CPU of the second sample is the difference of the two samples. RSS can count shared pages more than once.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--interval <ms>` | The time between two samples, from 250 to 60000. Default: 2000. |
| `--service <service>` | Only the processes of this service. |
| `--owner <kind>:<id>` | Only the processes of this owner. |
| `--tree` | Also show the unregistered descendants of the registered processes. |
| `--sort <key>` | Sort the processes by cpu, rss, pid, or service. Default: cpu. |
| `--no-storage` | Do not measure the storage. In the live view, the key d starts it. |
| `--no-mouse` | Do not turn on the mouse reports in the live view. The key m turns them on. |
| `--once` | Take two samples, print one text frame, and end. |
| `--json` | Print one JSON object (schema foundation-cli/0.1). |
| `--home <NUU-home>` | Read this NUU home. Default: $NUU_HOME, else ~/.nuucognition. |

## nuu

```text
nuu [options] [command]
```

NUU ecosystem meta CLI

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--verbose` | Show stack traces for errors |

Subcommands: `setup`, `login`, `logout`, `whoami`, `env`, `config`, `org`, `machine`, `home`, `places`, `client`, `resolve`, `session`, `station`, `topology`, `net`, `trace`, `styles`.

### nuu setup

```text
nuu setup [options]
```

Set up this machine: your Name, the machine display name, and the Git author (shared by every NUU CLI; needs no network)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <name>` | Your Name (the author of your notes) |
| `--machine-name <name>` | The branch prefix of this machine (default: from the hostname) |
| `--machine-proper-name <name>` | The machine display name (the name people read, such as "Ada Laptop") |
| `-y, --yes` | Ask no question. Every open input takes its default |
| `--json` | Output one JSON result envelope. Never asks a question |

### nuu login

```text
nuu login [options]
```

Log in to your NUU account. The account publishes shards (flint shard release). It does not put this machine in an org

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--verbose` | Show detailed progress output |

### nuu logout

```text
nuu logout [options]
```

Log out of your NUU account: revoke this machine's login and remove its session

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

### nuu whoami

```text
nuu whoami [options]
```

Show your Name, this machine, its org, your NUU account, and the enrollment of this machine

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

### nuu env

```text
nuu env [options] [command]
```

Show the build, the data mode, and the services that this CLI uses; switch a dev build between dev and prod data

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

Subcommands: `use`, `clear`.

#### nuu env use

```text
nuu env use [options] <mode>
```

Store the data mode of the dev builds of this machine: dev or prod

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `mode` | dev or prod |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### nuu env clear

```text
nuu env clear [options]
```

Remove the switch file: the dev builds of this machine use dev data again

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

### nuu config

```text
nuu config [options] [command]
```

The NUU config of this machine (name, machine-name, org, shared)

Subcommands: `name`, `machine-name`, `org`, `shared`.

#### nuu config name

```text
nuu config name [options] [name]
```

Get or set your Name: how you appear as the author of notes on this machine

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Name to store (e.g. "Nathan Luo") |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--unset` | Unset the stored name |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### nuu config machine-name

```text
nuu config machine-name [options] [name]
```

Get or set the branch prefix of this machine (machine-name): the prefix of worktree branches

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | The branch prefix to store |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--unset` | Unset the branch prefix; the hostname is the default |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### nuu config org

```text
nuu config org [options] [command]
```

The org of this machine: new Flints get it. Absent means local (no org)

Subcommands: `show`, `set`, `unset`.

##### nuu config org show

```text
nuu config org show [options]
```

Show the org of this machine

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

##### nuu config org set

```text
nuu config org set [options] <slug>
```

Set the org of this machine. The id comes from --id, the enrollment of this machine, or a Flint of this machine with that org

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `slug` | The org slug, for example nuucognition |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--id <uuid>` | The id of the org. The admin of the org gives it. Not needed when this machine knows the org |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

##### nuu config org unset

```text
nuu config org unset [options]
```

Remove the org of this machine: new Flints are local (no org). The Flints, the enrollment, and the account do not change

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### nuu config shared

```text
nuu config shared [options] [command]
```

Every key of the NUU config

Subcommands: `get`, `set`, `unset`.

##### nuu config shared get


Get a value of the NUU config

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `key` | Config key |

```text

```

Set a value of the NUU config

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `key` | Config key |
| `value` | A JSON number or true/false, else text |

##### nuu config shared unset

```text
nuu config shared unset [options] <key>
```

Unset a value of the NUU config

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `key` | Config key |

### nuu home

The NUU home: its version, its layout, and its migration

#### nuu home doctor

Sort every entry of the home into known, mirror, stale, foreign, unknown, and wrong-mode. Read only. Exit 1 when not clean

#### nuu home migrate

```text

```

Bring the home to the version this build needs: PLAN, CHECK, BACKUP, VERIFY BACKUP, APPLY. A refusal in CHECK means nothing happens

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Stop after CHECK: print the plan and every refusal, change nothing. Exit 1 when a real run would refuse. Works with --rollback too |
| `--json` | Output the result as JSON |
| `--rollback <dir>` | Restore the home from one backup directory (a path under `<home>`/backups) |
| `--break-lock` | Remove a stale migration lock. Refuses while its pid lives. With --dry-run it only reports |


Print every path of the home for this build

|---|---|

### nuu client

```text
nuu client [options] [command]
```

Run nclient, the NUU client of this machine

Subcommands: `serve`, `start`, `port`, `stop`, `status`, `install-service`, `enroll`.

#### nuu client serve

```text
nuu client serve [options]
```

Run the nclient daemon of this dev build in the foreground (home ~/.nuucognition/nclient-dev)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--port <port>` | Loopback port for the FIRST start of this home (then NCLIENT_PORT, then 13032). It is recorded; a later start must agree with it |
| `--allow-source` | Run the daemon from TypeScript source through tsx, in the foreground, to debug it. Never for a standing daemon: it warns every minute |
| `--no-cgroup-guard` | Run the daemon outside a systemd unit with MemoryMax and TasksMax. For a test or a debug session only: it warns every minute |

#### nuu client start

```text
nuu client start [options]
```

Start the nclient daemon of this dev build in the background (home ~/.nuucognition/nclient-dev, default port 13032)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--port <port>` | Loopback port for the FIRST start of this home (then NCLIENT_PORT, then 13032). It is recorded; a later start must agree with it |
| `--no-cgroup-guard` | With no unit installed: start the daemon outside a systemd unit. For a test or a debug session only; the daemon warns every minute |

#### nuu client port

```text
nuu client port [options]
```

Print the one daemon port of this home. The bound port is the lease of the home, and the recorded port is immutable in code. To change it by hand: stop the daemon (nuu client stop), delete ~/.nuucognition/nclient-dev/port, and start with --port `<n>`

#### nuu client stop

```text
nuu client stop [options]
```

Ask the nclient daemon to stop (POST /stop with the stop token of this home), and wait until it is gone. It first finishes the requests in progress. No signal is sent

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--force` | Kill the daemon (SIGKILL) when it is not gone after the wait, or when it hangs. Only with /proc evidence that the pid runs the nclient serve command of this installation and owns the listening socket of this home |
| `--wait <seconds>` | How long to wait for the requests in progress (default 155) |

#### nuu client status

```text
nuu client status [options]
```

Show whether the daemon runs, and the machine it answers for

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### nuu client install-service

```text
nuu client install-service [options]
```

Write, enable, and start the systemd user unit of this dev build, with memory and task limits (MemoryMax=1500M, TasksMax=96, KillMode=control-group). A standing daemon on a real machine always runs under it

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dev` | Confirm that you mean the DEV unit (nclient-dev.service). A dev build needs it; a prod build refuses it |
| `--uninstall` | Stop and disable the unit, and remove its file |
| `--dry-run` | Print the unit and the commands, and change nothing |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### nuu client enroll

```text
nuu client enroll [options]
```

Enroll this machine in one org of NUU Network with the enrollment secret of the org. No NUU account is needed

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--network <prod\|dev\|url>` | The network: prod, dev, or the URL of a deployment (default: the build mode of this CLI) |
| `--org <slug>` | The org, required: its slug or its id. The secret is the enrollment secret of THIS org |
| `--secret-file <path>` | The file with the enrollment secret of the org. The admin of the org gives it (default ~/.nuucognition/nclient-dev/secrets/enroll-secret) |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

Resolve an entity reference through nclient

|---|---|

| `--to <rung>` | The rung: ping or locate (default: "locate") |
| `--child-type <type>` | For children: keep only this type slug |
| `--json` | Output the request and the response as JSON |

#### nuu net org

```text
nuu net org [options] [command]
```

The org of NUU Network: refresh the cached org slug of this machine; admin only: make an org, or replace its enrollment secret (needs the bootstrap secret of the deployment). The orgs of your NUU account: nuu org list

Subcommands: `create`, `secret`, `refresh`.

##### nuu net org create

```text
nuu net org create [options] <slug>
```

Make one org. The org id is minted by the server and never changes; the slug is a cache that only the server admin corrects (orgs:renameSlug)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--title <title>` | The title, for example "NUU Cognition" |
| `--id <uuid>` | The org id that another deployment minted. One org has one id in every environment (default: a new id) |
| `--network <prod\|dev\|url>` | The network (default: the build mode of this CLI) |
| `--bootstrap-secret-file <path>` | The file that holds the bootstrap secret of the deployment |
| `--secret-out <path>` | Where the enrollment secret of the org is written, mode 600 (default ~/.nuucognition/nclient-dev/secrets/enroll-secret) |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |


| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--network <prod\|dev\|url>` | The network (default: the build mode of this CLI) |
| `--bootstrap-secret-file <path>` | The file that holds the bootstrap secret of the deployment |
| `--secret-out <path>` | Where the enrollment secret of the org is written, mode 600 (default ~/.nuucognition/nclient-dev/secrets/enroll-secret) |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |
