### nuu styles

```text
nuu styles [options] [pattern]
```

Show the output patterns of every NUU CLI: refusal, warning, success, key/value

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `pattern` | One pattern: refusal, warning, success, keyvalue |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Print the envelope of the pattern |

## Tools for Developers

These commands serve the development of Flint: journey tests, entity debugging, the Steel UI, Steel Plates, the MCP server, and the local server.

### flint entity

```text
flint entity [options] [command]
```

Debug surfaces for the entity modules: ask one place, print the roster

Subcommands: `ask`, `roster`, `answer`.

#### flint entity ask

```text
flint entity ask [options] <root> <verb> [ref]
```

Ask one Flint client at `<root>` one read verb about a reference

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `root` | The Flint root, . for the current one |
| `verb` | One of describe, lookup, locate, children |
| `ref` | An entity reference; optional for describe |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--to <rung>` | The rung: ping or locate (default: "locate") |
| `--child-type <type>` | For children: keep only this type slug |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### flint entity roster

```text
flint entity roster [options]
```

Print the places discovered from here and the source of each

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--json` | Output as JSON |

#### flint entity answer

```text
flint entity answer [options]
```

Answer one entity Request for one Flint: JSON on stdin, JSON on stdout

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <path>` | The Flint root that answers |

### flint plate

```text
flint plate [options] [command]
```

Manage Steel Plates

Subcommands: `list`, `install`, `update`, `reinstall`, `dev`, `info`.

#### flint plate list

```text
flint plate list [options]
```

List installed Plates

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--json` | Output as JSON |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint plate install

```text
flint plate install [options] <name-or-source>
```

Install a Plate from GitHub releases

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name-or-source` | Plate slug or GitHub owner/repo |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--version <version>` | Install a specific version |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint plate update

```text
flint plate update [options] [name]
```

Install the newest release of each Plate with no pin; a Plate with a version in flint.toml keeps it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Optional plate slug |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint plate reinstall

```text
flint plate reinstall [options] [name]
```

Reinstall Plates from GitHub releases (bypass version check)

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Optional plate slug |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint plate dev

```text
flint plate dev [options] <name> [source]
```

Point a Plate at a local dev source — a vite dev-server URL or a plate checkout directory

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Plate declaration name or manifest name |
| `source` | Dev source: http(s) URL of a plate dev server, or a directory containing plate.yaml |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--clear` | Clear the dev source (both URL and path) |
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint plate info

```text
flint plate info [options] <name>
```

Show Plate details

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Plate declaration name or manifest name |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

### flint orbtest

```text
flint orbtest [options] [command]
```

Journey testing: validate, run, diagnose, drift, regen, and document orbtest definitions

Subcommands: `init`, `validate`, `check`, `upgrade`, `run`, `drift`, `regen`, `doctor`, `skills`, `author`, `register`, `snapshot`, `approve`, `page`, `behaviour`, `coverage`, `profile`, `environment`, `step`, `task`, `story`, `suite`, `requirement`, `settings`, `services`, `secret`, `baseline`, `runs`, `brief`, `components`, `fixture`.

#### flint orbtest init

```text
flint orbtest init [options]
```

Scaffold an orbtest/ folder of format 0.0.1 for one platform family: orbtest.toml with the format stamp, one environment under environments/ and one UI profile with the same id under ui/profiles/, one step under ui/steps/, the requirements of that platform under requirements/, empty ui/tasks/, stories/ and ui/fixtures/ folders, and .gitignore. web: profile web-desktop-chromium, step nav.goto, requirements node, probe-tool, playwright-chromium. terminal: profile cli-terminal, step cli.run, requirements node, terminal-program, and no browser. desktop: profile desktop-app, step app.restart, requirements hyprland, wtype, grim, atspi. The output ends with the next commands, each with --root

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory that will hold orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--platform <platform>` | Platform family of the scaffold profile: web, terminal, desktop (default: web) |
| `--base-url <url>` | Web only. Root URL of the app under test (default: `http://127.0.0.1:<port>`) |
| `--port <n>` | Web only. Port for the default base URL (default: 3000); cannot be combined with --base-url |
| `--force` | Rewrite the scaffold files of the platform (or of the behaviour layer) when orbtest/ already exists; files outside the scaffold, including .local/, are left alone |
| `--layer <layer>` | The layer to scaffold: ui, behaviour (default ui). behaviour: orbtest.toml and .gitignore when orbtest/ is new, environments/local.yaml, stories/example.hello.yaml, and behaviour/specs/example.hello.md with one case that needs no test code; an existing orbtest/ keeps its orbtest.toml and .gitignore |

#### flint orbtest validate

```text
flint orbtest validate [options]
```

Check orbtest definitions: schemas, references, route patterns, profile register, sources, check types (dom, route, sql, command), snapshot suites, stories, and the behaviour layer (specs, cases, kinds, fixtures, places, sidecars; its findings are warnings, and --strict makes them exit 2)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--strict` | Count the findings of the behaviour layer as errors: exit 2 when there is one |

#### flint orbtest check

```text
flint orbtest check [options]
```

Describe an orbtest/ folder without loading it: the declared format against the format this CLI reads, and the folder against the layout of that format, the behaviour layer included (its findings are warnings; --strict makes them errors). Never writes.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the report as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |
| `--strict` | Count the findings of the behaviour layer as errors: exit 2 when there is one (default: warnings) |

#### flint orbtest upgrade

```text
flint orbtest upgrade [options]
```

Run the definitions migration chain from the format the folder declares to the format this CLI reads, then stamp it

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--to <format>` | Target format (default: 0.0.1) |
| `--dry-run` | Print the file operations and the notes, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest run

```text
flint orbtest run [options]
```

Execute journey tasks on a profile and write an append-only run record

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--profile <id>` | Profile to run on (default: the only registered profile) |
| `--task <id...>` | Restrict to these task ids; combines with --story as a union |
| `--story <id...>` | Restrict to the tasks of these story ids; combines with --task as a union |
| `--input <key=value...>` | Task input overrides |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--json` | Print the run record as JSON instead of a summary |
| `--agent <kind[:target]>` | Agent for agentic steps and verification, e.g. orbh or orbh:claude/s5m (default: profile.agent, then orbtest.toml [agent] target) |
| `--no-agent` | Run without an agent even if the profile declares one |
| `--keep-services` | Leave profile.setup.start services running after the run |
| `--jobs <n>` | Run tasks on up to `<n>` workers, each with its own services and browser; needs `parallel` on the profile (default 1) |
| `--keep <n>` | After this run, keep only the newest `<n>` run directories, counting this one (default: orbtest.toml [runs] keep, else keep all) |
| `--confirm` | Anchor every met task to the current commit and store each step's fingerprint (refuses with uncommitted changes) |
| `--freeze` | After a met run, convert each agentic step's trace into deterministic steps and write a freeze bundle at .local/runs/`<run-id>`/`<task-id>`/bundle.json for register |
| `--components <json>` | With --freeze: JSON map of locator description to repository path, as author takes it |
| `--watch` | Show the run on the current display: a browser window for a web profile, a terminal window that mirrors the screen for a terminal profile. One worker; never with --confirm; the record is the same |
| `--slow <ms>` | With --watch: milliseconds to wait before every browser action (default 250) |
| `--hold <ms>` | With --watch: milliseconds the window stays open after the run (default 3000) |

#### flint orbtest drift

```text
flint orbtest drift [options]
```

Detect drift: diff each step's component since its task's confirmed commit (a testid locator goes stale only when its value leaves the file), fold in run signals, and mark stale steps

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--profile <id>` | Restrict to tasks on this profile (default: every profile) |
| `--task <id...>` | Restrict to these task ids |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--json` | Print the drift report as JSON instead of a table |
| `--check` | Read-only gate: write nothing, need no browser, exit 1 when a blocking task is stale or unconfirmed |

#### flint orbtest regen

```text
flint orbtest regen [options]
```

Regenerate a stale step from its description, or re-author a stale task

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--step <id>` | Regenerate one step: replay its task up to the step, run the description agentically, compare |
| `--task <id>` | With --step: the task to replay (default: the first task on the profile that uses the step). Alone: re-author the task from its description |
| `--profile <id>` | Profile to replay on (default: the only registered profile) |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--agent <kind[:target]>` | Agent for agentic steps and verification, e.g. orbh or orbh:claude/s5m (default: profile.agent, then orbtest.toml [agent] target) |
| `--no-agent` | Run without an agent even if the profile declares one |
| `--input <key=value...>` | Task input overrides |
| `--json` | Print the review bundle as JSON |

#### flint orbtest doctor

```text
flint orbtest doctor [options]
```

Diagnose a profile: environment requirements, services, setup command, app version pin, browser, agent, data sources. Plain doctor starts no service: on a profile that declares setup.start or setup.local_start, a failing setup verify and the app version check are skipped, not failed, and the line names doctor --start. Exit 0 when every other check passes

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--profile <id>` | Profile to check (default: all registered) |
| `--start` | Start profile.setup.start services before checking, stop them after; setup verify and the app version check then run against them and fail for real |
| `--keep-services` | With --start, leave the services running afterwards |
| `--setup` | If checks fail, provision this machine from the requirements file (or the deprecated setup.script) through one Orbh session, then re-check |
| `--setup-target <runtime/profile>` | Orbh target for the provisioning session (default: profile.agent.target, then orbtest.toml [agent] target, then claude/o5m) |

#### flint orbtest skills

```text
flint orbtest skills [options]
```

Print agent-facing documentation generated from the installed schemas, registries, and command tree

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--section <name>` | Print one section: atoms, fixtures, locators, platforms, steps, tasks, stories, profiles, verification, checks, sources, suites, commands, run-layout, behaviour |
| `--json` | Print the documentation data as JSON instead of markdown |
| `--check` | Verify the output lists every registered atom, convention, locator strategy, check type, source provider, platform, command, and behaviour term, and that every schema field has a description; exit 1 otherwise |

#### flint orbtest author

```text
flint orbtest author [options]
```

Run a brief as one agentic task and write a review bundle under .local/runs/author-`<id>`/

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--brief <file>` | Brief YAML: description, profile, story, inputs, seed, verify |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--profile <id>` | Profile to run on (default: brief.profile, else the only registered profile) |
| `--input <key=value...>` | Brief input overrides |
| `--components <json>` | JSON map of locator description to repository path, e.g. \{"testid=browse-as-guest": "src/components/auth-gate.tsx", "*": "src/components/mesh-home/mesh-list.tsx"\} |
| `--from-run <dir>` | Convert the trace of an earlier author run instead of running the agent; rewrites that run's bundle.json |
| `--final-route <url>` | With --from-run: the final route when run.json is missing |
| `--agent <kind[:target]>` | Agent for the authoring step, e.g. orbh or orbh:claude/s5m (default: profile.agent, then orbtest.toml [agent] target) |
| `--no-agent` | Run without an agent even if the profile declares one |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--json` | Print the bundle as JSON instead of a summary |

#### flint orbtest register

```text
flint orbtest register [options]
```

Write a reviewed bundle into the definitions: an author bundle adds steps and a task, a freeze bundle replaces agentic steps of a task, a regen bundle sets fields (review only unless --approve)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--bundle <dir>` | Directory holding bundle.json, e.g. orbtest/.local/runs/author-`<id>` or orbtest/.local/runs/`<run-id>`/`<task-id>` |
| `--approve` | Write the definitions; without it register prints the diff and exits 2 |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |

#### flint orbtest snapshot

```text
flint orbtest snapshot [options]
```

Capture snapshot suites on their profiles and compare each cell with its approved baseline

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--suite <id...>` | Restrict to these suite ids (default: every suite) |
| `--profile <id>` | Restrict the matrix to one profile (default: every profile the selected suites name) |
| `--entry <id...>` | Restrict to these entry ids within the selected suites |
| `--input <key=value...>` | Suite input overrides |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--json` | Print the run record as JSON instead of a summary |
| `--agent <kind[:target]>` | Agent for agentic entry verification, e.g. orbh or orbh:claude/s5m (default: profile.agent) |
| `--no-agent` | Run without an agent even if the profile declares one |
| `--keep-services` | Leave profile.setup.start services running after the run |

#### flint orbtest approve

```text
flint orbtest approve [options]
```

Promote captures from a snapshot run to approved baselines, recording who approved them and when

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--run <id>` | Snapshot run id, the .local/runs/snapshot-`<time>` directory name (required) |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--cell <suite/entry/profile...>` | Approve only these cells (default: every new and failed cell in the run) |
| `--by <name>` | Approver name (default: the operator Name from `flint whoami`) |
| `--json` | Print the outcomes as JSON |

#### flint orbtest page

```text
flint orbtest page [options]
```

Write one static, read-only HTML page of the definitions: profiles, environments, stories, tasks, steps, suites, requirements, the behaviour layer (specs, cases, flags, newest runs, places, fixtures), and settings (default: orbtest/.local/runs/page/index.html)

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--out <path>` | Write the page to this file instead |
| `--open` | Open the page in the default browser ($BROWSER when set) |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs) |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest behaviour

```text
flint orbtest behaviour [options] [command]
```

Behaviour tests: one Markdown spec per behaviour under orbtest/behaviour/specs/, each `###` heading one case, bound to test code in any language by the marker `orbtest: <spec-id>/<case-id>`, run in an environment and a place, with one run record and one report

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `run`, `bind`, `drift`, `heal`, `fixture`, `place`.

##### flint orbtest behaviour list

```text
flint orbtest behaviour list [options]
```

List the cases of every behaviour spec: id, kind, the criteria each proves, and the flags the files decide (invalid, unbound, quarantined, waived, repaired, ungated). Writes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--story <id>` | Only the cases of the specs of this story |
| `--kind <kind>` | Only the cases of this kind |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour show

```text
flint orbtest behaviour show [options] <id>
```

Show one spec with its cases, or one case with its fields, gate, binding, flags, and resolved environment. Writes nothing.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | A spec id (flint.home.guard) or a case id (flint.home.guard/home-is-read-at-call-time) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour run

```text
flint orbtest behaviour run [options]
```

Run behaviour cases in their environment and place, write the run record and report.html under .local/runs/`<run-id>`/, and print `report <path>`. Exit 0 when no case of a blocking story failed, 1 when one ended fail, error, or budget_exceeded, 2 for a bad argument before any write, 3 when the place or the stage hook failed before any case.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--spec <id...>` | Only the cases of these specs. Each filter narrows the selection; with no filter, every case runs |
| `--case <id...>` | Only these cases (`<spec-id>`/`<case-id>`) |
| `--story <id...>` | Only the cases of the specs of these stories |
| `--kind <kind...>` | Only the cases of these kinds |
| `--environment <id>` | Run every case in this environment (wins over the case, the spec, and orbtest.toml) |
| `--place <id>` | Run in this place (wins over the place of the environment) |
| `--jobs <n>` | Run cases on up to `<n>` workers (default 1) |
| `--confirm` | Anchor each passed deterministic case to the current commit in its sidecar (refuses a dirty tree) |
| `--keep-scratch` | Keep the case folders, the container, or the ssh run folder after the run |
| `--resume <run-id>` | Continue an earlier run from its completed cases; the selection of that run stays |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour bind

```text
flint orbtest behaviour bind [options]
```

Read the `orbtest: <spec-id>/<case-id>` markers of the test files and compare them with the sidecars in behaviour/bindings/. With no flag, report and write nothing. --scan writes the sidecars and keeps every anchor. --check writes nothing and exits 1 when a sidecar is not current, a marker names no case, or a case has two markers.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--scan` | Write one sidecar per spec under behaviour/bindings/; anchors stay |
| `--check` | Write nothing; exit 1 when a sidecar is not current, a marker names no case, or a case has two markers |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour drift

```text
flint orbtest behaviour drift [options]
```

Mark each anchored case stale or suspect when its words, its bound test, a component, or a proven criterion changed after the anchor. Writes nothing. --check exits 1 when a case of a blocking story is stale or unconfirmed.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--check` | Gate: exit 1 when a case of a blocking story is stale or unconfirmed |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour heal

```text
flint orbtest behaviour heal [options]
```

Give each case of a run that is not green one verdict (environment_broken, test_wrong, flaky, spec_outdated, product_bug, unknown) with a rationale that cites evidence, and write heal/`<case>`.json in the run folder. It never weakens an assertion, never changes the words or the product, and applies a test patch only with --apply.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--run <run-id>` | The behaviour run to heal (default: the newest behaviour run) |
| `--case <id...>` | Only these cases (default: every case of the run that is not green) |
| `--apply` | Apply a test_wrong patch that passes every guard rule; the case is then repaired and waits for review |
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | The Orbh agent target of the healer, e.g. claude/o55xh |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour fixture

```text
flint orbtest behaviour fixture [options] [command]
```

List, build, and author the fixtures of the behaviour layer: recipes or scripts that build real state

Subcommands: `list`, `build`, `author`.

###### flint orbtest behaviour fixture list

```text
flint orbtest behaviour fixture list [options]
```

List the fixtures of behaviour/fixtures/: id, form (recipe or script), the fixtures it uses, the cache mode, and the number of verify checks. Writes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

###### flint orbtest behaviour fixture build

```text
flint orbtest behaviour fixture build [options] <id>
```

Build one fixture and its uses chain in a scratch case folder of the place, then run its verify checks. Exit 0 when it builds and verifies, 1 when a build, materialize, or verify step fails.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Fixture id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--place <id>` | Build in this place (default: the place of the environment, else local) |
| `--environment <id>` | Build in this environment (default: orbtest.toml [behaviour] environment, else local) |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

###### flint orbtest behaviour fixture author

```text
flint orbtest behaviour fixture author [options]
```

Ask an Orbh agent to propose a fixture recipe for a state in words. The proposal, the prompt, and the reply go to .local/runs/fixture-author-`<time>`/ and nothing is written under behaviour/fixtures/: a person approves the recipe first. Exit 0 with a proposal, 1 when the reply holds no valid recipe, 3 when no agent answered.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--brief <text>` | The state that the fixture must build, in words |
| `--id <id>` | The id of the proposed fixture (default: fixture) |
| `--target <runtime/profile>` | The Orbh agent target (default: orbtest.toml [agent] target, else claude/o5m) |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest behaviour place

```text
flint orbtest behaviour place [options] [command]
```

List, show, and check the places where an environment runs: local, ssh, docker, or command

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `check`.

###### flint orbtest behaviour place list

```text
flint orbtest behaviour place list [options]
```

List the places: the built-in local place, places/`<id>`.yaml, and .local/places/`<id>`.yaml (a machine-local place wins). Writes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

###### flint orbtest behaviour place show

```text
flint orbtest behaviour place show [options] <id>
```

Show one place with every key of its file. Writes nothing.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Place id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

###### flint orbtest behaviour place check

```text
flint orbtest behaviour place check [options] <id>
```

Check that the place is reachable and has its tools: its check op, else an open, one probe command, and a close. Exit 0 when every check passes, 3 when one fails.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Place id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest coverage

```text
flint orbtest coverage [options]
```

Show a state for each criterion of each story (proven, failing, stale, gap, not-run, waived), from the behaviour cases and the UI tasks that name it, and list the orphan cases, the test files that carry no case, and the quarantined and repaired cases. Writes nothing.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--story <id>` | Only this story |
| `--layer <layer>` | Which layers prove a criterion: ui, behaviour, all (default all) |
| `--root <dir>` | Product root: the directory that holds orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print one JSON value on stdout; progress and human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest profile

```text
flint orbtest profile [options] [command]
```

List, show, and change the profiles: under format "0.0.1" one file per profile in ui/profiles/, the driver half, which names its environment in environments/; the profiles.yaml register in the legacy flat layout

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `add`, `set`, `unset`, `rename`, `remove`.

##### flint orbtest profile list

```text
flint orbtest profile list [options]
```

List every profile: environment, platform, tier, agent target, and how many tasks and suites use it

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest profile show

```text
flint orbtest profile show [options] <id>
```

Show one profile: the resolved profile, the tasks and suites that use it, its requirements, and the resolved agent target. Under format "0.0.1" the fields are the driver half and the environment half joined, and both files are named.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Profile id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest profile add

```text
flint orbtest profile add [options] <id> [assignment...]
```

Add a profile. Under format "0.0.1" it writes the driver half to ui/profiles/`<id>`.yaml and, unless --environment names an environment that exists, the environment half to environments/`<id>`.yaml with the same id; in the legacy flat layout it writes to the profiles.yaml register. Start from the defaults of a platform (--platform), a copy of the driver half of a profile, which names the same environment (--from), or a whole profile from a file (--file). Assignments after the id change the new profile before the write.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Id of the new profile |
| `assignment` | Fields to set on the new profile, as `<path>`=`<value>`, e.g. base_url=http://127.0.0.1:4180; a field of the environment half goes to the new environment |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--platform <platform>` | Start from the defaults of a platform: web, terminal, desktop |
| `--from <id>` | Start from a copy of the driver half of an existing profile; the new profile names the same environment |
| `--file <path>` | Read the whole profile from a JSON or YAML file; its environment fields become the new environment |
| `--environment <id>` | Name an environment that exists instead of writing a new one (format "0.0.1" only) |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest profile set

```text
flint orbtest profile set [options] <id> <assignment...>
```

Set one or more fields of a profile. The value is YAML. agent.target is checked against the Orbh profiles; a profile with no agent block gets agent.kind orbh. Under format "0.0.1" this is the driver half: a field of the environment half is refused with the environment set command that owns it, and environment=`<id>` names another environment.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Profile id |
| `assignment` | One or more `<path>`=`<value>`, e.g. agent.target=claude/o5m viewport.width=1440; with --file, one bare `<path>` |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--file <path>` | Read the value of the one `<path>` from a JSON or YAML file |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest profile unset

```text
flint orbtest profile unset [options] <id> <path>
```

Remove one field from a profile. Under format "0.0.1" this is the driver half; it removes a field of the environment half only when the UI profile file holds it by mistake, and names environment unset otherwise.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Profile id |
| `path` | Dotted field path, e.g. agent.target |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest profile rename

```text
flint orbtest profile rename [options] <old> <new>
```

Rename a profile. Rewrites task.profile, task.steps[].on, suite.profiles[], and profile: in ui/briefs/*.yaml, and renames its ui/baselines/`<suite>`/`<entry>`/`<old>`.png and .json. The environment keeps its id; rename it with environment rename.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `old` | Current profile id |
| `new` | New profile id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest profile remove

```text
flint orbtest profile remove [options] <id>
```

Remove a profile: its driver half under ui/profiles/ in format "0.0.1", its entry in the profiles.yaml register in the legacy flat layout. Refused while a task or a suite uses it; each user is named. The environment stays; remove it with environment remove when no other profile names it.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Profile id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

```text
flint orbtest environment [options] [command]
```

List, show, and change the environments: the environment half of a profile, one file per environment in environments/ under format "0.0.1"

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `add`, `set`, `unset`, `rename`, `remove`.

|---|---|

##### flint orbtest environment add

```text
flint orbtest environment add [options] <id> [assignment...]
```

Add an environment to environments/`<id>`.yaml. Start from the environment half of the defaults of a platform (--platform), a copy of an environment (--from), a whole environment from a file (--file), or, with none of the three, the id alone: a behaviour environment needs no other field, e.g. `environment add scratch place=local`. Assignments after the id change it before the write. A profile names it with `profile set <profile> environment=<id>`, or `profile add <profile> --environment <id>`; a behaviour spec or case names it with `environment: <id>`.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Id of the new environment |
| `assignment` | Fields to set on the new environment, as `<path>`=`<value>`, e.g. base_url=http://127.0.0.1:4180 |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--platform <platform>` | Start from the environment half of the defaults of a platform: web, terminal, desktop |
| `--from <id>` | Start from a copy of an existing environment |
| `--file <path>` | Read the whole environment from a JSON or YAML file |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest environment set

```text
flint orbtest environment set [options] <id> <assignment...>
```

Set one or more fields of an environment. The value is YAML. A field of the UI profile is refused with the profile set command that owns it. Every profile that names the environment sees the change.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Environment id |
| `assignment` | One or more `<path>`=`<value>`, e.g. base_url=http://127.0.0.1:4180 setup.verify="curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:4180"; with --file, one bare `<path>` |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--file <path>` | Read the value of the one `<path>` from a JSON or YAML file |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest environment unset

```text
flint orbtest environment unset [options] <id> <path>
```

Remove one field from an environment. A field of the UI profile is removed only when the environment file holds it by mistake; otherwise the profile unset command that owns it is named.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Environment id |
| `path` | Dotted field path, e.g. version_check |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest environment rename

```text
flint orbtest environment rename [options] <old> <new>
```

Rename an environment. Rewrites the environment field of every UI profile that names it. A file environments/`<old>`.yaml that holds only this environment becomes environments/`<new>`.yaml. A requirement that names the old id in its profiles field is not rewritten: when no profile has the old id, the rename is refused and names the requirement; change it first with requirement set.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `old` | Current environment id |
| `new` | New environment id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

```text
flint orbtest environment remove [options] <id>
```

Remove an environment: its file under environments/. Refused while a profile names it; each profile is named.

|---|---|
| `id` | Environment id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

```text
flint orbtest task check [options] [command]
```

Edit the deterministic checks of the final verify of a task

Subcommands: `add`, `list`, `remove`.

###### flint orbtest task check add

```text
flint orbtest task check add [options] <id>
```

Append one deterministic check to the final verify of a task. A verify that is one mapping becomes a list that keeps the entry. The check goes into the first deterministic entry, or into a new deterministic entry when there is none

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Task id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--kind <type>` | Check type: dom, route, sql, or command (any registered type) |
| `--arg <path=value...>` | One field of the check, e.g. pattern=/mesh/:id or locator.strategy=role. The value is parsed as YAML |
| `--file <path>` | Read the fields of the check from a JSON or YAML mapping; --arg values override it |
| `--verify <n>` | Append to this 0-based deterministic verify entry (default: the first deterministic entry) |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

```text
flint orbtest task check remove [options] <id> <n>
```

|---|---|

##### flint orbtest task negative

```text
flint orbtest task negative [options] [command]
```

Create negative controls: tasks with expect: fail

Subcommands: `add`.

###### flint orbtest task negative add

```text
flint orbtest task negative add [options] <source-id>
```

Create a negative control: a copy of a task with the profile, story, seed, pacing, steps, and verify of the source, one wrong input, and expect: fail. The file is ui/tasks/`<id>`.yaml

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `source-id` | Id of the task to copy |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--input <key=value>` | The one input to replace and its wrong value. The value is parsed as YAML |
| `--id <id>` | Id of the new task (default: `<source-id>`.wrong-`<key>`) |
| `--name <text>` | Name of the new task (default: "\<source name> with the wrong `<key>`") |
| `--description <text>` | Description of the new task (default: a negative-control sentence) |
| `--verify <n>` | Keep only this 0-based deterministic verify entry, as one mapping (default: copy verify as it is) |
| `--without-story` | Do not copy the story of the source task |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

```text
flint orbtest story [options] [command]
```

List, show, and change the stories in stories/*.yaml

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `add`, `set`, `unset`, `rename`, `remove`, `criterion`.

##### flint orbtest story list

```text
flint orbtest story list [options]
```

List stories: tier, acceptance criteria, and bound tasks

| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest story show

```text
flint orbtest story show [options] <id>
```

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest story add

```text
flint orbtest story add [options] <id>
```

Create a story in stories/`<prefix>`.yaml, where the prefix is the id up to the first dot. The file is created or appended to.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--name <text>` | Short human title of the feature |
| `--story <text>` | The user story: as a `<role>`, I want `<goal>`, so that `<benefit>` |
| `--tier <tier>` | Gate tier: blocking or informational |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest story set

```text
flint orbtest story set [options] <id> <assignments...>
```

Change fields of a story: `<path>`=`<value>`, the value parsed as YAML, e.g. stale=true or tier=informational. With --file, give one bare `<path>`.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--file <path>` | Read the value of the one `<path>` from a JSON or YAML file |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest story unset

```text
flint orbtest story unset [options] <id> <path>
```

Delete one field of a story, e.g. tier or acceptance.1

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest story rename

```text
flint orbtest story rename [options] <old> <new>
```

Change the id of a story and rewrite task.story in every task and story: in every brief that names it. A file stories/`<old>`.yaml that holds only this story becomes stories/`<new>`.yaml; any other file keeps its name. Refused when the target file exists.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest story remove

```text
flint orbtest story remove [options] <id>
```

Delete a story. Refused while a task names it in task.story; --unbind first removes story from those tasks.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--unbind` | Remove the story field from every task bound to the story, then delete the story |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

```text
flint orbtest requirement [options] [command]
```

List, show, and change the machine requirements: one file per requirement in requirements/ under format "0.0.1", requirements.yaml and the files named by profile.requirements in the legacy flat layout

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `add`, `set`, `unset`, `remove`.

##### flint orbtest requirement list

```text
flint orbtest requirement list [options]
```

List every machine requirement: its file, its platforms, and the profiles it applies to

| Option | Description |
|---|---|

| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest requirement show

```text
flint orbtest requirement show [options] <id>
```

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Requirement id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--file <path>` | The file that holds the id, relative to the definitions root. Only the legacy flat layout needs it, where two requirements files may use one id |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest requirement add

```text
flint orbtest requirement add [options] <id> [assignment...]
```

Add a requirement: to requirements/`<id>`.yaml under format "0.0.1", to requirements.yaml in the legacy flat layout, or to the --in file in either. name, check, and guidance are required. The file is created when it is absent.

|---|---|
| `id` | Id of the new requirement, unique in its file |
| `assignment` | Fields as `<path>`=`<value>`, e.g. name="Node.js 22" check="node --version" guidance="Install Node.js 22." |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--in <file>` | Requirements file, relative to the definitions root. Format "0.0.1": a file in requirements/ (default: requirements/`<id>`.yaml). Legacy flat layout: a requirements file (default: requirements.yaml, or the one loaded file that holds the id) |
| `--file <path>` | Read the whole requirement from a JSON or YAML file |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--in <file>` | Requirements file, relative to the definitions root. Format "0.0.1": a file in requirements/ (default: requirements/`<id>`.yaml). Legacy flat layout: a requirements file (default: requirements.yaml, or the one loaded file that holds the id) |
| `--file <path>` | Read the value of the one `<path>` from a JSON or YAML file |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest secret

```text
flint orbtest secret [options] [command]
```

Manage the names in .local/secrets; a human enters the values in a local browser page

Subcommands: `list`, `add`, `enter`.

##### flint orbtest secret list

```text
flint orbtest secret list [options]
```

List each env:// name the definitions reference, and each name in .local/secrets, with its state: set, empty, or missing. A folder that has not moved yet is read from .secrets-orbtest too, as the harness reads it. Never prints a value.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest secret add

```text
flint orbtest secret add [options] <name...>
```

Add names to .local/secrets with an empty value. A name already in .local/secrets or in .secrets-orbtest is left as it is. The agent adds names only; the human enters the values with `secret enter`.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | One or more environment variable names, e.g. ONYX_EMAIL |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--dry-run` | Print the names the command would add, and write nothing |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest secret enter

```text
flint orbtest secret enter [options] [name...]
```

Ask the human for secret values in a local browser page, and write them to .local/secrets. Serves one form on 127.0.0.1 with a one-time token, accepts one submit, then stops. Prints only the names it set. Refused while .local/secrets is not gitignored.

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Names to ask for (default: every referenced or listed name with no value) |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--timeout <ms>` | Stop and write nothing after this many milliseconds (default: 600000) |
| `--no-open` | Do not open a browser; print the URL only |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

| `profile` | Only this profile of the entry |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest baseline prune

```text
flint orbtest baseline prune [options]
```

Delete the baselines whose suite, entry, or profile no longer exists in the definitions

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest runs

```text
flint orbtest runs [options] [command]
```

Delete old run folders under .local/runs/

Subcommands: `prune`.

##### flint orbtest runs prune

```text
flint orbtest runs prune [options]
```

Delete old run folders of each kind: task runs (`<run-id>`), author-*, snapshot-*, doctor-*, and behaviour-*. The newest --keep of each kind stay. A snapshot run that a baseline names in its run field always stays, and a behaviour run that a sidecar anchor names always stays; neither counts against --keep.

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--kind <kind>` | Only this kind: run, author, snapshot, doctor, behaviour (default: every kind) |
| `--keep <n>` | Folders to keep per kind, newest first, 0 or more (default: [runs] keep of orbtest.toml) |
| `--runs <dir>` | Run record directory (default: `<orbtest>`/.local/runs); it must be inside the definitions root |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest brief

```text
flint orbtest brief [options] [command]
```

Write and read the briefs under ui/briefs/, the input of author --brief

Subcommands: `list`, `show`, `add`, `set`, `unset`, `rename`, `remove`.

##### flint orbtest brief list

```text
flint orbtest brief list [options]
```

List every brief with its profile, story, component map, and problems

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest brief show

```text
flint orbtest brief show [options] <id>
```

Print a brief, its resolved id and name, its component map, and its problems

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id: the file name under ui/briefs/ without .yaml |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest brief add

```text
flint orbtest brief add [options] <id>
```

Write a new brief to ui/briefs/`<id>`.yaml

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id; the task id author proposes. Written to ui/briefs/`<id>`.yaml |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--description <text>` | What the user does and what must be true at the end. Required. May hold \{\{inputs.name\}\} placeholders |
| `--name <text>` | Task name (default: the first sentence of the description) |
| `--profile <id>` | Profile to run on (default: the only registered profile) |
| `--story <id>` | Story of the task author proposes |
| `--seed <id>` | Seed applied before the run (default: the default seed) |
| `--input <key=value...>` | Task inputs, as author --input. A value may be env://NAME |
| `--verify-file <path>` | JSON or YAML file holding the final verification, in a task's verify shape |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest brief set

```text
flint orbtest brief set [options] <id> <assignment...>
```

Set one or more fields of a brief. The value is parsed as YAML

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id |
| `assignment` | One or more `<path>`=`<value>`, e.g. description="..." or inputs.title=Sapiens; with --file, one bare `<path>` |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--file <path>` | JSON or YAML file holding the value of the one `<path>`, e.g. verify |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest brief unset

```text
flint orbtest brief unset [options] <id> <path...>
```

Delete one or more fields of a brief

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id |
| `path` | One or more field paths to delete, e.g. seed or inputs.title |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest brief rename

```text
flint orbtest brief rename [options] <old> <new>
```

Change the id of a brief. ui/briefs/`<old>`.yaml and ui/briefs/`<old>`.components.json move to the new id together, and an id field in the brief follows

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `old` | Current brief id |
| `new` | New brief id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest brief remove

```text
flint orbtest brief remove [options] <id>
```

Delete ui/briefs/`<id>`.yaml and its component map ui/briefs/`<id>`.components.json

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest components

```text
flint orbtest components [options] [command]
```

Write the component map of a brief, ui/briefs/`<id>`.components.json, the input of author --components

Subcommands: `set`, `remove`.

##### flint orbtest components set

```text
flint orbtest components set [options] <id> <locator> <path>
```

Map one locator description to the file that renders it. Refuses a path that does not exist

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id |
| `locator` | Locator description, e.g. 'label "Email"', as in external:web:`<locator>` in bundle.json; * is the fallback |
| `path` | File that renders the element, relative to the product root. It must exist in the repository |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest components remove

```text
flint orbtest components remove [options] <id> <locator>
```

Delete one entry of the component map of a brief. A map left empty is deleted

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Brief id |
| `locator` | Locator description of the entry to delete |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

#### flint orbtest fixture

```text
flint orbtest fixture [options] [command]
```

Manage the files under ui/fixtures/ that upload steps and task inputs name as ui/fixtures/`<name>`

Subcommands: `add`, `list`, `remove`.

##### flint orbtest fixture add

```text
flint orbtest fixture add [options] <source>
```

Copy a file into ui/fixtures/. Name it in an upload step or a task input as ui/fixtures/`<name>`

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `source` | File to copy into ui/fixtures/ |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--as <name>` | Name under ui/fixtures/ (default: the file name of `<source>`); may hold subfolders, e.g. imports/orbtest-import.md |
| `--force` | Replace a fixture of the same name |
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest fixture list

```text
flint orbtest fixture list [options]
```

List the files under ui/fixtures/ with their size and the definitions that name them

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

##### flint orbtest fixture remove

```text
flint orbtest fixture remove [options] <name>
```

Delete a file under ui/fixtures/. Refuses while a task input, a step, a suite entry, or a brief names it

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Fixture name, e.g. attachment.txt or ui/fixtures/attachment.txt |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--root <dir>` | Directory holding orbtest/ (default: cwd) |
| `--dry-run` | Print a unified diff of each file the change would write, and write nothing |
| `--json` | Print the result as JSON on stdout; human lines go to stderr |

### flint server

```text
flint server [options] [command]
```

Manage the local Flint server

Subcommands: `start`, `daemon`, `dev`, `status`, `stop`, `list`.

#### flint server start

```text
flint server start [options]
```

Start the Flint server

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --port <port>` | Port to bind in --ephemeral mode (default: the first free port from 14040) |
| `--path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--steel-url <url>` | Paired Steel host URL for cascading shutdown |
| `--ephemeral` | Run unregistered (no registry/discovery) — for a dev instance parallel to a live server |
| `--dev-plate <name=url>` | In-memory dev-plate override (repeatable), e.g. tasks=http://127.0.0.1:13501 (default: []) |
| `--dev-origin <origin>` | Approve the origin of a web client of this --ephemeral server, such as the Steel of `steel dev --ephemeral` (repeatable; `http://127.0.0.1:<port>` or `http://localhost:<port>`) (default: []) |

#### flint server daemon

```text
flint server daemon [options]
```

Run a Blacksmith-spawned Flint server daemon

#### flint server dev

```text
flint server dev [options]
```

Start the server in dev mode with auto-reload on source changes

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --port <port>` | Port to bind |
| `--path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

#### flint server status

```text
flint server status [options]
```

Check server status

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--server <url>` | Server URL |
| `--wide` | Render full-width table output without narrow stacking |

#### flint server stop

```text
flint server stop [options]
```

Stop the Flint server

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--server <url>` | Server URL |

#### flint server list

```text
flint server list [options]
```

List all running Flint servers from the shared registry

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--wide` | Render full-width key/value output |

### flint steel

```text
flint steel [options] [command]
```

Serve the Steel host, and manage the Steel UI state (.steel/ directory)

Subcommands: `serve`, `preferences`, `state`, `boards`.

#### flint steel serve

```text
flint steel serve [options]
```

Serve the Steel host from the Steel build of this CLI. --machine: one host for every Flint of this computer (`http://<flint>.localhost:<port>/`), the mode that Blacksmith runs

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `--machine` | Serve every Flint of this computer from one port (default 13035) |
| `--port <port>` | The port of the Steel host |
| `--host <host>` | The host to bind to (default 127.0.0.1) |
| `--server <url>` | Without --machine: the URL of the Flint server (default: found or started for the Flint of --path) |
| `--path <dir>` | Without --machine: the path of the Flint (default: the Flint of the current folder) |
| `--no-open` | Do not open the browser |

#### flint steel preferences

```text
flint steel preferences [options]
```

Show Steel preferences

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--reset` | Clear all Steel preferences |

#### flint steel state

```text
flint steel state [options] <plate-name>
```

Show plate state

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `plate-name` | Name of the plate |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |
| `--reset` | Clear all state for this plate |

#### flint steel boards

```text
flint steel boards [options] <plate-name>
```

List board/view configs for a plate

| Argument | Description |
|---|---|
| `plate-name` | Name of the plate |

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to flint (default: auto-detect) |

### flint mcp

```text
flint mcp [options] [command]
```

Expose the capabilities of this Flint as an MCP server

Subcommands: `serve`.

#### flint mcp serve

```text
flint mcp serve [options]
```

Start the Flint MCP stdio server

| Option | Description |
|---|---|
| `-p, --path <dir>` | Path to Flint workspace (default: auto-detect) |
| `--server <url>` | Use a running Flint server URL instead of direct filesystem access |
